# vpn0wn QA Test Plan

Last updated: 2026-10-21 (Europe/Paris).

This document tracks the automated and manual QA coverage required for the vpn0wn Android redesign.

The layer lists below are target requirements, not a passing coverage report. [Source-inspected coverage](#source-inspected-coverage) inventories current source, including explicitly identified pending changes; [development evidence](development.md#recorded-verification) records actual executions and their scope. The original documentation audit at `11d4a9b1` ran no runtime suites. #13 adds focused detail-fragment lifecycle tests; broader rendered-row, instrumented navigation and screenshot suites remain absent.

## Selected-network hostname recovery controls — #73/#74

2026-10-19 (Europe/Paris): source delivered in [PR #300](https://github.com/novakin/vpn0wn-android/pull/300) after independent ultra review and passing [final-head CI](https://github.com/novakin/vpn0wn-android/actions/runs/39205527275) at `57f0d3ea`. The local results below retain their original scope; installed acceptance remains separate. `GoBackendRebindTest` adds selected-Network startup/refresh, saved-profile/TUN/session/local-port preservation, numeric zero-DNS, LEGACY refusal to guess a resolver, unavailable resolution, stale activation/transport, interrupted completion and retired startup authority. `TunnelRecoveryTest` exercises an actual owned backend DNS outage followed by a valid fresh recovery without a new cooldown, asserts zero native entries/charges/unknown settlements for lookup failure, and retains the four-request cap across repeated outages. `RecoveryDiagnosticsBindingTest` includes the fixed DNS-failure category. `RecoveryReadoutsLayoutTest` renders native-view output for a DNS failure without a native outcome in English, French and RTL at normal/large text, preserving the existing fonts and read-only row. The Settings preview includes the hostname option alongside the existing numeric/default fixtures. `SettingsAutomationUiTest` selects an actual hostname profile through the existing chooser and checks durable selection, no implicit Enable and Cancel preservation. Native `tests/handshake_test.go` extends real WireGuard engine controls with one-peer IPv4-to-IPv6 endpoint replacement, preserved unrelated peer/TUN/local port and rejected stale generation/non-numeric/changed-port replacements; existing claims, cancellation, throttle, static identity, teardown and send controls remain required.

Focused backend/chooser/controller/automation and recovery-layout checks pass 137 tests in ten suites, with zero failures, errors or skips and zero Detekt findings. The first attempt failed test compilation because a fixture called a hidden framework method; existing Robolectric shadow setters corrected that fixture. The next attempt passed compilation but failed one of 135 tests because an old hostname-only fixture started automation without an eligible Network. Corrected ordinary-LEGACY startup and a separate no-Network negative control preserve the production admission boundary. Original failures remain retained. Host Go 1.26.9 race checks pass for conn/device, UAPI, protected bind, overlays and handle registry; JNI context/capture/denial/failure/lifetime controls pass. Final-head CI passed for PR #300 at the revision linked above; The later [API36 bootstrap record](reviews/2026-10-19-hostname-lockdown-bootstrap-acceptance.md) separates scoped native/packet evidence from its original checker failure; wider native hostname behavior remains pending.

Independent source and native-view reviews are satisfied within this local scope. Fresh Settings output shows hostname membership and the existing actions. DNS-result output is complete in English at 320×640, French at 320×640/200% text, and RTL at 640×320/200%, with no native outcome or fabricated transport readiness. Day/night captures retain the same fixed palette. Enlarged French/RTL Settings captures omit the changed helper, and the final French chooser capture is scrolled; assertions independently verify every choice is reachable with a 48dp target. These synthetic views do not establish installed dialog geometry, IME or TalkBack speech. No fonts, tokens, layout or view owner changes.

The separate [API36 startup/lockdown record](reviews/2026-10-19-hostname-lockdown-bootstrap-acceptance.md) covers selected-Network DNS and a bounded ordinary-UID canary, with original failed aggregate and corrected offline reanalysis kept distinct. The 2026-10-21 [API36 recovery/cancellation record](reviews/2026-10-21-hostname-recovery-lockdown-acceptance.md) adds a fresh passing run: 30 native stages, natural hostname/numeric exhaustion, one admitted socket refresh per activation with retained session/TUN/port and authenticated HTTP, zero endpoint-name DNS in the numeric window, held-DNS cancellation without admission/resurrection, fresh authenticated activation and all 18 cleanup checks. Independent runtime review is recorded in that report; no product source changes. External resolver paths, family reachability, natural network change, Android resolver cancellation latency and physical/OEM acceptance remain open. No DPI, battery, speed or full #73/#74/#75 closure follows from this source slice.

## Test Layers

### Optional AmneziaWG controls — #76

2026-10-19 (Europe/Paris), combined source verification before merge: AWG checkpoint `b87501e3` was integrated with delivered [PR #300](https://github.com/novakin/vpn0wn-android/pull/300) (`33e5479b`). `GoBackendRebindTest.ownedAmneziaHostnamePreservesExplicitZeroKeepalive/ownedAmneziaHostnamePreservesRangeKeepalive` checks AWG dispatch, retained interface fields, numeric startup/refresh, unchanged saved profile and retained activation/TUN establishment. `TestHandleHostnameRecoveryAcrossEngines` runs the same shared-handle contract against real WireGuard and AWG devices: replacement, stale generation, non-numeric/changed-port refusal before mutation, retry of an unclaimed episode, unrelated peer/local-port preservation and refusal to reuse a claimed episode. AWG exhaustion uses a host-only overlay fixture and the actual initiation/timer owners. At staged tree `3a930ce17417f72c9585df50e0dd8f688b60bd83`, the combined JVM run passes 98 controls across `GoBackendRebindTest`, `NetworkAutomationSettlementTest` and `TunnelRecoveryTest`, with zero failures/errors/skips and zero Detekt findings. WSL/OpenJDK 21 and Go 1.26.9 host checks pass all six race groups plus JNI context/capture/denial/failure/lifetime controls. The fresh four-ABI debug assembly passes; artifact audit and APK signature verification pass. This APK is 40,183,554 bytes, SHA256 `8c53a06345c5319e059686dac1ccf337244d4da5d651787aa621e1d2f6a13cd3`, retains API24, includes all sixteen expected native libraries and both pinned engines, and carries source-exact license assets. Retained logs are `20261019-081324-613-34568d2420c7408bb7a5a9b3508f9949.log` (JVM), `20261019-082039-318-dffb111b697f4dfea25d30e8e4c1c177.log` (native), `20261019-082122-580-dcff5b3472844cea8586d1ee399c3d5b.log` (APK) and `20261019-082309-946-53899253457d43c9850a02b6e61164a5.log` (artifact/signature). At committed source `2300a1f458e212da81fcd3cc5f167566fb70b8a9`, source CI passes. The separate [A4 native lifecycle record](reviews/2026-10-19-amneziawg-native-lifecycle-acceptance.md) accepts twelve passing API24 x86_64 stages and all nine host cleanup/preservation checks on an isolated probe APK after independent ultra review; final PR documentation/checks remain separate gates. All earlier results below retain their original source/artifact scopes; the previous APK and complete audit are preserved.

Preliminary independent ultra review found three source defects before compilation: real native batches could send initiation without a post-I/J cancellation check; editor reconstruction validated retained settings against default MTU instead of a valid lower configured MTU; and the ordinary nonempty attribute parser rejected admitted empty I1–I5 chains. Corrections add an explicit engine-owned initiation boundary with a captured socket generation, immutable editor retention with final actual-MTU validation, and narrow empty-I admission. Independent source rereview accepted those corrections. Controls include native-engine cancellation with BatchSize 128, generation replacement, unchanged ordinary batching, MTU576/S4=64800 editor/recreation and invalid edited-MTU boundaries, and full configuration/proxy empty-I round trips. The subsequent source delta received independent ultra review; the actual JVM results and remaining Android gates follow below.

2026-10-19 (Europe/Paris): the initial Gradle attempt passed all 31 tunnel config/crypto tests, including eight AWG controls, then stopped at the keepalive formatter ReturnCount check (`20261019-073040-232-09a1773d909d47468902e322123d966e.log`). A readable two-return formatter resolved that finding. The next run passed zero-finding Detekt and executed 149 UI tests with five failures (`20261019-073531-622-ae27341f0b9e4e9891653f946a1994a8.log`): a replacement-service fixture after explicit DOWN, two binding cases lacking the Application owner, and actual Editor/Detail range clipping. These attempts remain failed. The successful controls cover configuration round trips, protocol intent, malformed/overflow inputs, ordinary WireGuard compatibility, editor/Parcelable retention and kernel refusal before privileged work.

The correction run passed 14 tests across `GoBackendHandshakeTest` (8), `AmneziaKeepaliveBindingTest` (4) and both new `LocalizedCopyLayoutTest.amnezia*` methods, with zero failures/errors/skips and zero Detekt findings; terminal exit 0 after 386,107 ms (`20261019-074647-968-916d86b206c84a17801d36cf12697155.log`). The fixture now creates the replacement service required after DOWN; the binding fixture reuses the existing Application test environment. A field-specific binding adapter applies wrapping after input type, and only Detail keepalive removes single-line truncation. The output controls exercise native input connection, complete numeric/range/disabled text, copy/accessibility metadata and English/French/RTL compact/enlarged day/night output. Independent source review accepts the affected correction. Author inspection of the fresh originals is satisfied with complete range/disabled output and preserved fonts/tokens; independent review is also satisfied with eight sampled fresh originals across the three configurations and both themes. Installed IME/TalkBack remain separately identified gates. The passing focused rerun does not reclassify the earlier failed 149-test run.

The first Go preparation attempt checksum-verified and patched the existing Go 1.26.9 archive, then failed `go mod tidy` because its generated toolchain was placed inside a traversed module subdirectory. The task-owned generated tree was moved outside the module; dependency resolution and both overlays then passed (`20261019-070744-763-e354546cbdba47cdb05db71782f023ea.log`). The original preparation failure remains in `20261019-063900-893-4c1919e7cdb647119707a9614bdc3db3.log`.

The first compiled host run found two failures: clamped-identical synthetic private keys prevented a fixture peer from being admitted, and the selected AWG engine captured S4 before its initial blocking TUN read, losing the first transport packet when configuration later supplied nonzero padding. The fixture now uses distinct clamped keys. An AWG-only exact-source overlay refreshes padding after Read and relocates the payload only when its offset changed, preserving packet bytes and bounding the destination buffer. A deterministic TUN-read barrier precedes configuration in both real loopback engines. The first-run log `20261019-070919-216-b6309bca1b6f4e2e98400bae401b3339.log` and diagnostic run `20261019-071033-739-35f60ade9bda4c4b9f23998d04b9f093.log` retain those failures; no production logging was added.

The corrected WSL Go 1.26.9 host gate passes race-enabled WireGuard descriptor/protection and handshake fixtures, UAPI, androidbind, overlay and handle-registry groups. AWG controls pass real configured I/J/S/H handshake and first-packet/bidirectional traffic, retained-port protected rebind, default-enabled cookies, unchanged ordinary batching, same-generation cancellation before initiation and protection refusal. No one-byte WG warm-up appears in the AWG train. The combined command then stopped only because JAVA_HOME was unset (`20261019-071216-410-6e97d5d1d2a740a799cf0520023f7e72.log`). A separate explicit-JDK21 host JNI run passes seven legacy plus capture callback/denial/failure/lifetime controls; Android-arm64 package resolution also passes (`20261019-071302-125-e86dd8a7e6ed462e8446059d133ca99b.log`). Independent source review accepted the compiler/fixture/padding corrections. Those host commands ran no Android APK or device producer; later Gradle evidence is recorded separately above.

The first APK attempt failed before native compilation because this linked worktree had not initialized its two pinned native submodules (`20261019-075336-107-f744e5f809cb4a29889a8ba799612829.log`). Initializing those exact gitlinks resolved the checkout failure without changing product code. The retry built all four supported ABIs successfully, terminal exit 0 after 176,124 ms (`20261019-075540-123-415982b87dc743fca3bd35668eb4547c.log`). The debug APK is 32,022,072 bytes, SHA256 `9fe72ff593dde6e4743ffe0d847f76942a551a76cc7974e377ffec51ca60c9d0`; its actual manifest retains minimum API24 and the debug app identity. Static inspection finds all sixteen expected native libraries, both selected engines in each of the four `libwg-go.so` module records, and source-exact AWG MIT/credits assets. Independent static APK inspection confirms its manifest identity/API floor, four ELF architectures, construction/query/close symbols for both engines and the five JNI exports in each combined library, plus source-exact notice inclusion. This is compilation and artifact inclusion, not installed API24 loading or Android transport acceptance.

The [A4 cohort](reviews/2026-10-19-amneziawg-native-lifecycle-acceptance.md) records API24 loading, activation/query/rebind/teardown and cleanup on its isolated APK. The separate [A2 retry1 traffic record](reviews/2026-10-19-amneziawg-native-lifecycle-acceptance.md#api24-a2-retry1-traffic--2026-10-19) accepts nine native stages, exact HTTP/fresh handshakes in both matching phases, wrong-H1 timeout with positive TX and zero handshake/RX/no peer request, and all eleven cleanup checks against the unmodified official v3.1.20260828 Windows peer. Independent ultra runtime and cleanup reviews pass; earlier traffic failures retain their original results and unproved causes. The separate [API36 A2 traffic record](reviews/2026-10-19-amneziawg-native-lifecycle-acceptance.md#api36-a2-traffic--2026-10-20) adds nine passing stages and eleven cleanup checks on API36 x86_64 with a public instrumentation shell observer; ordinary product UID and prior cohort boundaries remain.

The [2026-10-21 padding/header/rebind cohort](reviews/2026-10-21-amneziawg-padding-rebind-acceptance.md) adds thirteen API36 stages: configured nonzero S1–S4/content padding and header protection, matching/wrong-header-key/matching traffic, and exact authenticated responses after two retained-session socket rebinds. All eleven cleanup checks pass. This measures operation under those settings, not every emitted byte or first-packet delivery. Remaining acceptance includes physical/external-server and wider traffic matrices, network loss/handover, observed protection returns/timing, hostname/lockdown and recovery behavior, wider platforms/distribution obligations, installed UI/accessibility and applicable final-head review/checks. Host controls and each scoped Android cohort retain their distinct source/artifact limits. Existing Config/import/export and kernel WireGuard behavior must remain. A live server deployment or DPI-efficacy test is not authorized by engine selection.

### Planned network automation controls — #74

2026-10-19 (Europe/Paris), bounded source delivery and native acceptance: [PR #298](https://github.com/novakin/vpn0wn-android/pull/298) merged the first numeric/endpoint-free single-userspace slice as `1a084d016e440eb374ea2a6d9e1b7cc0a0022e42`. The [native A7 record](reviews/2026-10-19-network-automation-native-acceptance.md) accepts one isolated API28 x86_64 cohort at source `19667395800a7305a79e1a513d0596f3b3c858e3`: OWNED startup, explicit Disable to fresh protected LEGACY sockets, ordinary rebind, sampled identity/config preservation, synthetic traffic checkpoints, obsolete/pre-entry-interrupted refusal, DOWN/Resume boundaries and all fifteen cleanup/preservation facts. Its [published independent runtime review](https://github.com/novakin/vpn0wn-android/pull/298#pullrequestreview-5567387815) is satisfied within that scope. Enabled Resume is direct-controller only; physical routing, packets, in-flight cancellation, wider platform/lifecycle and installed UI/accessibility remain open under #74. Native A2–A6 remain failed. The PR owns final source review/CI; the historical checkpoints below retain their original revisions and limits. Their JVM A5–A7 labels identify separate runs from native A2–A7.

2026-10-19 (Europe/Paris), separate [API36 Settings Resume/Stop acceptance](reviews/2026-10-19-network-automation-native-acceptance.md#api36-settings-resume-and-stop-acknowledgement--2026-10-19): R4 retry1 passes actual Settings/service delivery (`lastStartId` 3→4 on the same monitor), manual DOWN preservation, Stop pause, explicit durable acknowledgement, ordinary process death/reentry, newer Stop, Disable/profile removal and checked cleanup. Independent state review covers all 21 preference payloads and full Stop-stamp correlation; original-pixel review is satisfied with eight dark portrait English/LTR 1080×1920 Settings captures. Earlier R1–R4 failures remain retained. This narrows installed acceptance only for that cohort; failed commits/history/permissions, sticky/boot/OEM behavior, traffic/routing and broader output/accessibility stay open. A7's enabled Resume remains direct-controller evidence.

Integrated CI run `39166732921` executed 1,555 UI tests with nine failures: `TunnelRenameRestorationTest.upRenamePreservesUnrelatedRunningNamesAndFreshManagerRestoration` exposed per-profile restore writes removing a later pending name, while eight `ZipExporterPolicyTest` controls joined or selected long-lived Settings observer jobs as though they were finite exports. The candidate repair admits a revalidated restoration batch without admitting later profiles after caller cancellation, and records steady view jobs separately from export jobs. Existing import-snapshot, restoration-policy, ZIP restriction/output and view-cancellation assertions remain; a new between-profile cancellation control checks admitted reconciliation and no later activation. Exporter production behavior and Settings UI/layout are unchanged; no UI roadmap change follows this fixture repair.

On 2026-10-18 (Europe/Paris), base `3d912cec` plus the uncommitted source/test repair passed 57 focused JVM tests across `AppDataTransferManagerTest` (23), `NetworkAutomationAdmissionTest` (6), `NetworkAutomationDisablePersistenceTest` (4), `TunnelRenameRestorationTest` (7), `TunnelStartupPolicyTest` (8) and `ZipExporterPolicyTest` (9), with zero failures/errors/skips and terminal exit 0 in 333,473 ms. The standalone Detekt command passed with zero findings and terminal exit 0 in 17,993 ms. Both used WSL Debian, OpenJDK 21.0.10, Gradle 9.8.1 and the existing Linux SDK, offline with two workers; all 675 tracked inputs remained unchanged through both commands. Retained task logs are `20261018-234800-011-7b653038322b40c0a502b5b7ca4ba216.log` and `20261018-235407-785-49b00155f1cf4ad4b79c135a64d45072.log` under ignored `.artifacts/codex-command-logs`; XML, receipts and input hashes remain in the isolated CI worktree. Final-head CI and installed/native acceptance remain separate. The frozen A1 native source/artifact retain their original revision and do not cover this restoration repair.

2026-10-18 (Europe/Paris), first focused Android command exited 1 after 27 seconds at Detekt with eighteen findings; its 42 source/document inputs remained unchanged. Kotlin/UI test execution did not complete. The retained command/log preserves this failed gate; authored corrections do not turn it into a pass. A subsequent gate must compile/run the selected tests separately, then execute standalone zero-finding Detekt on unchanged inputs.

2026-10-18 (Europe/Paris), A5 passed all 23 manager-transfer tests with zero failures/errors/skips in 273,082 ms; all 43 frozen inputs remained unchanged. A6 then executed 196 tests with 13 failures, zero errors/skips and terminal exit 1 in 327,413 ms on unchanged inputs. It retained 280 automation PNGs and passed the nine automation UI methods. Remaining failures identify detached queued Settings resume context, a missing native-version fixture shadow, an API28/JDK route-constructor fixture incompatibility and ten recovery controls sampling newly pending durable-policy reads. These are scoped execution results, not a passing integrated gate.

The corrected A7 candidate passed all 196 tests across the same thirteen classes, with zero failures/errors/skips and terminal exit 0 in 532,835 ms; all 45 inputs remained unchanged. The nine automation UI methods generated 280 fresh originals across the eight configurations below, including actual enabled/disabled title colors and unchanged warning summaries. This accepts the corrected local JVM/native-view scope; standalone Detekt also passes with zero findings in 17,633 ms. Final integrated-head/CI acceptance and installed routing remain separate. Earlier failed runs retain their original outcomes.

The recovery fixture correction awaits the existing `awaitSnapshot { !requestPending }` completion signal for eleven dropped/disabled/limit assertions affected by the ten failures. Expected statuses, counters, native-call counts, synthetic clock and existing five-second bound remain unchanged; no production recovery change or generic pump is introduced. The Legacy capture-lifetime fixture drops its incidental, unasserted AllowedIPs route to avoid API28/JDK `RouteInfo` incompatibility; its address, cached hostname, manual Legacy UP, completed Disable, zero replacement and subsequent ready rebind assertions remain. This does not change production routing or establish routing acceptance. A7 passes the Settings resume/shadow and disabled-title corrections with fresh output; preserve the failed A6 evidence and final-head review boundary.

Full Disable regression now asserts a failed newer DOWN's marker stays retired after real captured Legacy hand-back while VPN/session remain UP, and that a fresh explicit ordinary manual UP can establish new intent. A blocked old-manual settlement control checks marker retirement before final conversion and again afterward. Those cases close the gap between first-transaction crash/reopen proof and non-manual settlement; A7 passes these scoped controls as recorded above.

Additional correction controls include `NetworkAutomationDisablePersistenceTest` reopening the real store immediately after the first opt-out transaction, preserving unrelated/exact-live valid UP markers while rejecting failed DOWN and no-live-proof origins; service tests keep callbacks/observer/receipts on initial commit failure and preserve active Resume hold/failure without retry. Expanded settlement controls exercise live non-default manual handover/loss/network return and obsolete ownership, older failed startup versus newer manual UP, same-default capture reopen and already-Legacy Disable capture reuse. Prerequisite controls cover identical-profile metadata-only mode import and serialized Enable/mode-change ordering. A7 passes the scoped JVM controls as recorded above; that execution remains separate from installed/device acceptance.

Correction controls also recreate the controller/model and real preference store for the permitted completed/resolved default manual-UP restoration versus the forbidden missing non-default counterpart. A failed Disable on a newly started unregistered service must release that inert foreground lifetime while preserving durable enabled state. `NetworkAutomationPrerequisiteTest` contributes five metadata/import and both-order admission controls, all passing in A7.

The candidate now also includes `NetworkAutomationSettlementTest` for automatic/final-Disable write failure, `NetworkMonitorServiceTest` for overlapping start/attach and stale failure, expanded `GoBackendRebindTest` lifetime/publication/snapshot barriers and `SettingsAutomationUiTest` for actual controls plus synthetic native output. The output matrix covers English 320×640 and 411×891 at normal scale, French 640×320 at 2× scale and Arabic RTL 320×640 at 2× scale in day/night themes, fourteen status readouts/action rows, default chooser and the complete Disable warning/buttons. Output is configured under ignored `.artifacts/settings-automation-previews` (`VPN0WN_AUTOMATION_OUTPUT` override). Initial execution and its failure/output limits are recorded above; A7 passes the corrected tests and generates fresh output. Standalone Detekt passes with zero findings. Author and independent ultra reviews are satisfied with this synthetic native-view output; the four applied UI skills are UI/UX Pro Max, Interface Design, Design System and Frontend Design. Final integration, installed WindowManager/IME behavior, keyboard/TalkBack and real routing remain separate gates.

2026-10-18 (Europe/Paris), later frozen host checkpoint: `test-host.sh` passed six race-enabled Go groups (`conn`, `device`, `uapiserver`, `androidbind`, overlay and handle ownership) and the expanded host JNI controls under `-Xcheck:jni`, including actual capture Prepare/Report/Confirm order, each denial/failure stage, original failure identity, destroyed-service refusal and worker global-reference release. The run includes lifecycle-serialized snapshots and captured-socket drain barriers. Terminal/quiet exit was 0 in 28,762 ms; all 27 participating Go/C/JNI/harness input hashes match before/after. This adds scoped host evidence only; Java `GoBackend`, Android compilation/static/UI, final integrated head, CI and installed behavior remain unverified.

**Required controls; first source slice delivered, wider acceptance open.** The [selected decision](architecture.md#select-background-network-ownership--74-2026-10-18), [first implementation boundary](backend-contracts.md#first-implementation-boundary--74) and [operator limits](operations.md#planned-network-automation--74) own policy. [PR #297](https://github.com/novakin/vpn0wn-android/pull/297) delivered policy at `a7c57049`; its independent design review added no runtime proof. PR #298 separately delivered source, with its recorded review/checks and separately bounded API28 A7 and API36 Resume/Stop acceptance above. The following matrix still governs remaining work and affected regressions; passing one source or native cohort does not fulfil the entire matrix.

Saved-mode repair controls must use actual Go userspace with a disabled import retaining `multiple_tunnels=true`: prove the existing checked native row identifies saved kernel state, native OFF commits through manager admission, false hides the row and a valid selected default can reach Enable. Retain negative Go-ON, normal kernel true/false and recreation controls; failed reads preserve the last value and disable untrusted repair. Inspect complete title/summary/switch output at supported short, French 2×, RTL and day/night sizes without font shrinking or weakened clipping checks. A7 passes these controls and generates their fresh native-view output; installed acceptance remains separate.

2026-10-18 (Europe/Paris), limited earlier host checkpoint: root-owned execution of a frozen Go/C source copy passed six race-enabled Go groups and seven existing JNI controls. It preceded later native capture/snapshot/publication corrections and did not cover the new capture JNI path. It is not final-source verification or Android/JVM/static/output/device/CI evidence. Final command results and reviewed revision belong in the current PR acceptance record; no passing integrated result is recorded here.

| Boundary | Required controls |
| --- | --- |
| Selection | Validated current/newly losing mobile versus non-losing captive Wi-Fi; equal-quality advisory preference, all-losing retention/new candidate, transport ties/current/direct-default disambiguation, actual loss/block/suspension and fallback protective UP without validation. Read failure drops positive quality, not protection. No probe/request/timer/arrival-order shortcut. |
| Cause/manual arbitration | Equivalent admitted app/tile/remote UP/DOWN/TOGGLE/no-op/failure; pre-admission consent/remote denial creates none. A bound→B observed/queued→newer manual→old/reissued B cannot clear hold. Callback-intake frontier includes received/unprocessed events. Quality/advisory/filter-loss/re-entry with unchanged association, missing/delayed/reordered caps and coalescing remains non-releasing even with different N/new sequence. Independent-cause fixtures do not prove a production seam; unknown/opaque/recreated baselines preserve holds. Assert desired N, actual mode/N, evidence, epoch and receipt separately. |
| Durable ownership | Real store recreation and initial/final failed commits; unresolved TOGGLE; fresh episode versus plain Resume; current automatic PENDING/FAILED suppression across UP/handover/restore/adoption/restart and no quality-change reset of failed UP. Enabled config/name/delete/import fences and same-name replacement; failures retain unresolved bindings. Ordinary disabled mutations/restoration add no mandatory automation fence/receipt write; disabled rename/config/import followed by Enable must resolve actual current target/config without old receipt authority. Missing/incompatible target fails, no substitution. No transfer auto-enable/receipt transplant or guessed rollback. Manual DOWN/application restoration versus system policy, stale snapshot after lock wait and no last-used substitution. |
| Settings/consent | Actual default selection and Enable/status/Resume/Disable owners; hostname/numeric/endpoint-free selection with selected-network DNS, rejected kernel/multiple/other-running state, VPN consent Cancel/stale/recreated callbacks and no admission before consent. Notification denial preserves legal monitor/in-app Disable. Read-only status distinguishes monitoring/binding from health, manual hold/Stop/unresolved/failure and pending versus proved same-live exit. Disable confirmation discloses gap/failure; no persisted switch can claim asynchronous success. Native output at supported sizes/themes, enlarged text/RTL and keyboard/TalkBack remains separate. |
| Lifecycle/permissions | Installed API24/28/29/31/33/34/36 start/type/blocked/policy gates, notification denial and current foreground specialUse declaration; no location/SSID request in the first slice. Stop→acknowledged Resume→legitimate sticky/boot ignores consumed exit; newer Stop latches, vanished/ambiguous history preserves pause, failed acknowledgement resumes nothing. No automatic resurrection after force-stop or illegal-start failure; ordinary VPN DOWN leaves monitor lifetime independent. |
| Later exact-rule boundary | Exact Wi-Fi/OFF/physical rules are absent from the first slice. Later acceptance must prove precise/location-services/background gates and public same-N WifiInfo versus hidden/null/redacted/global info. Opaque VCN same-handle/internal path change and unsupported physical cellular/Ethernet rules never become trust or manual-hold-release evidence. No current source check delivers this wider boundary. |
| Native publication/handover | Same immutable capture for every real family inside actual expected-session native admission; duplicate→bind→final protect→duplicate close; all-new-original closure on partial failure. Exact-service `[N]`/empty reporting versus readiness, cleanup ownership and both orders of real invalidation/publication decision. No JNI manager/state-lock acquisition; close blocked IO before network lock, retained runtime port/TUN/session/peer/config and no owned-failure unbound fallback. |
| Explicit Disable | Atomic disabled+retired+PENDING before one Legacy fresh protection-only conversion/null report; initial write failure causes no conversion or monitor stop. Actual Legacy plus failed final save gets record-only reconciliation, no native replay. Busy retains pin; post-close family/protect/port/report failure exposes unavailable/unknown with automation off/manual reconnect. Resume, repeated Disable, Settings/service recreation and #73 recovery cannot retry incomplete conversion. New Enable waits for drain/reconciliation then creates a fresh episode. Assert successful same-TUN/session ON and no pin independently of saved status; manual Disconnect/Connect is a new activation, not successful hand-back proof. |
| Cancellation/recovery | Cancel before/after durable admission, native entry/report/publication, and concurrent DOWN/reconnect/no-op/config/name/backend/revoke/destruction; preserve original result/Throwable before dispatch, join/drain/reconcile outside needed locks before propagation. Old completion cannot touch fresh same-name activation. Day-request captures sample connecting presentation and night-request captures sample live presentation; this caption matrix does not independently cross every state with every theme or prove motion. **Author UI/UX verdict: satisfied with the bounded caption correction** using the four relevant skills, preserving approved hierarchy, packaged font roles, colors and drawing geometry. ### CI coverage and APK boundary

The resource-backed UI suite uses a 2 GiB test-worker maximum heap under [the development recipe](development.md#setup-and-source-boundaries). [#179](https://github.com/novakin/vpn0wn-android/issues/179) tracks the observed 1 GiB heap exhaustion and a separate Recovery entry timeout. Resource corrections require fresh full-suite and final-head CI checks with existing assertions, deadlines and input budgets intact; worker capacity does not prove a leak fix or APK runtime-memory improvement.

The [Android workflow](../.github/workflows/android.yml) executes all UI/tunnel debug unit tests, targeted release `ApplicationBackendLifecycleTest` startup control and Detekt on the actual PR head, `master` pushes and manual dispatch. Check the final-head run and debug/release XML/Detekt reports; a declared workflow or old passing revision is insufficient. Reports expire after seven days and may be unavailable after setup failure. The [development guide](development.md#android-ci-and-debug-apk-artifacts) owns toolchain, cancellation, artifact provenance and signing/install instructions.

PR/push runs and default manual dispatch do not assemble an APK. Explicit `build_apk=true` dispatch runs checks before debug assembly/upload; authorized artifact/install execution is recorded in the [G4 investigation](reviews/2026-10-07-g4-preflight-investigation.md) and [candidate-device follow-up](reviews/2026-10-07-g4-candidate-device-review.md). CI does not run instrumented/device, benchmark, native VPN/root or live/destructive VPS checks. It does not establish the support matrix below or configure branch protection. Keep #28/#34/#35/#36 physical acceptance open independently of CI success.

### Host kernel cleanup checks — #165

Run `python3 -m unittest discover -s scripts/tests -p 'test_wg_quick_cleanup.py'` on Linux/WSL. The test applies the same maintained patch as CMake, compiles actual production cleanup functions and records harmless command requests. Pair literal `a.b`/`a+b`, mixed valid metacharacters, plain and maximum-length names with colliding firewall comments and route tables; cover IPv4/IPv6, no-owned-table rejection and end/whitespace delimiters. Preserve owned cleanup and reject unrelated selection. Native ABI compilation and patch-triggered reconfiguration are separate build checks; host controls do not establish Android side effects. Any real collision test needs a wholly disposable Android guest with verified root, kernel WireGuard, firewall and netd support; an Android network namespace does not contain netd destruction.

### Host native Go tests

Run `bash tunnel/tools/libwg-go/test-host.sh` in the configured Linux/macOS/WSL native toolchain. Set `JAVA_HOME` to the host JDK and provide a host C compiler. The script prepares pinned Go 1.26.9 through the existing Makefile, generates the version-checked staged overlay and private modfile, and runs the UAPI, Android-bind, overlay and production handle-owner tests with `-race -count=1 -timeout=90s`. It then runs the JNI host harness with `-Xcheck:jni`. Controls use the production Unix-listener helper and cover idle accepted clients, complete handler drain, partial requests through the real pinned WireGuard handler, concurrent admission/repeated close, active-path refusal, stale-path/private-mode recovery, unlink-on-close and preservation of non-socket paths. Generator controls reject original replacements/unexpected pins, retain original cached source bytes and remove stale generated source before refresh. CI runs this host suite in addition to JVM/static checks. It does not compile Android native ABIs, assemble an APK or establish device/network behavior. For build-owner changes, separately compile the affected Android ABIs and verify an incremental source edit changes the library without APK assembly; [executed #66 proof](development.md#native-uapi-lifecycle-and-incremental-builds--2026-10-07) retains its original scope.

### Native handshake observation controls — #73 draft

The [first source contract](backend-contracts.md#native-handshake-observations--73-draft) requires controls through the actual overlaid protocol owners: initiation attempt/retry, local send success/failure, authenticated completion for initiator/responder and the existing exhausted-retry branch. Cover multiple peers, endpoint-free profiles, observed IPv4/IPv6 numeric endpoints and unknown attribution/family honestly. Confirm idle creates no failure event and protocol retry timing remains unchanged; preserve protection-before-send, same-generation warm-up, explicit/absent/zero ListenPort and terminal teardown controls. JNI controls must cover attached worker delivery, exceptions, concurrent teardown and early/failed startup.

For [#141](https://github.com/novakin/vpn0wn-android/issues/141), reproduce Android's numeric-parser rejection of bracketed URI hosts. `InetEndpointTest` and `GoBackendHandshakeTest` must use a numeric shadow that rejects brackets: `[::1]:51891` parses to bare host `::1`, preserves endpoint serialization and supports IPv6 observation family detection. Cover expanded IPv6, accepted raw scope suffixes, rejected-scope fallback retaining the original bracketed URI host, IPv4/hostname behavior, port 0/65535, config/userspace round trips and existing parse errors. A JDK-backed shadow that accepts brackets misses this platform boundary. Preserve genuine malformed numeric-callback rejection and startup-failure discard. These are source regression requirements; a reviewed candidate's Android activation, authenticated traffic and restoration need separate runtime evidence.

Close the actual native `Device`, including concurrent closure after ATTEMPT and before `SendBuffers`, and verify its nil no-op reaches no socket send and creates no SENT. The shared seam must suppress observations after terminal closure even while Java's activation can still be active; preserve the upstream nil return and timer behavior. Native TUN-read closure and Java service retirement are distinct owners.

Backend/model controls require at most five staged event kinds per configured peer, coalesced Handler callbacks outside every backend/native/context lock, activation IDs independent of backend instance/statistics invalidation, startup-success publication, failed-startup discard, null versus active empty diagnostics, distinct latest attempt/send/authentication/exhaustion retention, and obsolete events before and after Main dispatch during DOWN/reconnect/service destruction. Verify sequence ordering, honest coalescing gaps without inferred counters and monotonic observation time. Unknown/unconfigured peer events must not become per-peer health or grow staging storage. Run focused lifecycle/statistics/model suites before full UI/tunnel unit tests and Detekt; the actual final-head PR/CI record owns executions. This section declares required coverage, not passing test counts or publication evidence.

At this first observation-slice boundary, hostname refresh, family fallback, automatic recovery and network automation were separate #73/#74 work. Later [bounded recovery controls](#proposed-recovery-controls--73) and the [delivered first #74 slice and remaining controls](#planned-network-automation-controls--74) retain their own acceptance. The [retained-TUN rebind primitive](backend-contracts.md#retained-tun-userspace-rebind--73) has separate controls below. Future leak/packet tests must enforce [fail-closed lockdown bootstrap](operations.md#recovery-and-bootstrap-policy--73-draft). [Private API 36 Android acceptance](reviews/2026-10-10-native-handshake-avd-acceptance.md) records actual protection entry, single-peer ATTEMPT/SENT/AUTHENTICATED model facts, manual DOWN/replacement and corrected forced screen-off idle/private wake reachability, separately from failed instrumentation and debugger timing. Unavailable packet/physical/handover/family gates stay explicit; private standard-peer HTTP is not Internet reachability. The Detail presenter has separate source/output acceptance below.

Source checks actual root configuration and settled pre-draw, complete glyph/layout bounds and non-ellipsized values, packaged Mono roles, native line-by-line scrolling, footer reachability and read-only nodes. `VPN0WN_RECOVERY_READOUTS_OUTPUT` retains original top/middle/bottom and long-result captures when explicitly supplied. The first renderer invocation passes its line reach/fit assertions and retains 54 originals, represented by 25 distinct images, under the primary checkout's ignored `.artifacts/continuation-20261017/repair-244-248/recovery-readouts/native-attempt-01/`. Output is not accepted: capture positions omit the remaining long French result/failure text even though all line reach/fit assertions pass. A capture-only helper correction then passed a fresh one-test renderer rerun and executed zero-finding Detekt under `native-attempt-02/` beside that first evidence directory. Its 44-path source receipt matches before/after execution. The corrected producer retains 62 originals represented by 29 distinct images; the renderer author inspected every original through that distinct-image set, and the lead author additionally inspected eight key originals. Complete French pending/inhibited text spans four lines and result/category text spans nine lines across the retained captures. Both author reviews are satisfied within this synthetic matrix, with no product issue found. The independent reviewer separately inspected all 29 distinct original images, verified the one-test/zero-finding Detekt result and unchanged 44-path receipt, and is satisfied with that scoped output. This manual native-view result establishes no installed, IME, TalkBack or production-readiness acceptance. ### Native rebind controls — #73

Through the actual overlaid `Device.Rebind`, block a captured send and prove socket cancellation precedes the network-lock wait. Test partial UAPI lock ownership and concurrent rebind rejection before mutation, recovery after ordinary protection failure, endpoint-free profiles, explicit/automatic port intent, retained native device/TUN and terminal shutdown during protection. Close `Device` without `Bind.Shutdown` to cover native terminal state while Java's handle may still exist. Handle controls must prove removal stops admission and Device/JNI remain owned until the rebind borrow drains.

Retained-TUN assertions must observe `Close` on the actual `tun.Device` passed to `device.NewDevice`. The fixture wraps that device with an atomic close counter; `TestQuietDeviceTracksTUNClosure` proves real `Device.Close` advances it from zero to one. `ChannelTUN.Inbound` is not a closure signal: the pinned implementation leaves that channel open after TUN closure.

Linux `androidbind/socket_linux_test.go` uses the actual `StdNetBind` through the production Android bind owner. Independent IPv4/IPv6 contenders explicitly disable `SO_REUSEADDR`/`SO_REUSEPORT`, require IPv6-only sockets and distinguish `EADDRINUSE` from other failures. Both families must collide while open and reacquire the exact port after Close, Shutdown, partial protection denial, Device.Down and Device.Close. Rebind/retry controls retain the actual port and TUN, retire old descriptor access, and preserve explicit/omitted/direct-zero UAPI intent; explicit startup must also retain the requested port. Synthetic initiation bytes sent through Bind.Send check packet order and source-port continuity before/after replacement for both families. They are not a real protocol handshake or authenticated traffic. The full host race/JNI gate passed on 2026-10-12 (Europe/Paris); the [dated clarification](reviews/2026-10-10-native-rebind-acceptance.md#port-release-evidence-clarification--2026-10-12) retains the host-only checkpoint and historical descriptor-retirement scope.

The later [rooted port-lifecycle trial](reviews/2026-10-12-udp-port-release-acceptance.md) adds actual separate-UID production-backend IPv4/IPv6 exclusive collision/reuse for explicit, omitted and imported-zero profiles, retained-port/TUN/session rebind, genuine Android VPN Settings service destruction and same-process fresh activation. Its package-path variant and peerless profiles prove no shipping-APK behavior, warm-up packets or authenticated traffic. For a repeat, pin artifact/UID/namespace and exact socket tuples; require no reuse and distinguish EADDRINUSE from other failures. A stopService request is not destruction. Preserve original apps/configs/kernel state and original authorization modes; after the last activation use exact-companion system Disconnect before removal so a normal DOWN/uninstall does not leave it prepared. Check owned resources/paths, prepared package and final captured state separately from inactive cached capability/history metadata. No app/root-manager or all-device sign-off follows from these sampled controls.

JVM controls must invoke actual `GoBackend.rebind` with injected JNI responses: retain config, descriptor, handle and handshake session; reject inactive/wrong tunnel, stale service and same-tunnel replacement; race DOWN/destruction with blocked JNI outside `stateLock`; distinguish busy/unavailable from I/O failure; preserve exception/Error identity. Verify pre-admission and post-settlement interruption, including simultaneous stale identity. Reflectively exercise actual `VpnService.isNativeServiceActive` on an interrupted retained caller, interrupted originating startup thread and destroyed service. Pair that owner test with the host JNI same-context interrupted protection/observation and retry harness; a synthetic callback alone cannot prove the production Java check.

Run focused lifecycle/rebind/statistics/handshake tests and host race/JNI controls first, then full UI/tunnel unit tests, zero-issue Detekt and all four Android native ABIs for this cross-boundary operation. Record actual executions in the PR, with final-head review/CI. Native compilation does not assemble an APK. Separately authorized Android acceptance must invoke the primitive while retaining the same TUN/session, observe successful protected socket replacement and authenticated traffic, exercise failed protection and manual DOWN/destruction, and retain original device/network settings. VPN-backed Wi-Fi can establish scoped behavior; it cannot provide an independent direct/VPN or DPI comparison. [Rooted Android acceptance](reviews/2026-10-10-native-rebind-acceptance.md) records successful platform protection returns, retained-TUN/session UDP replacement and synthetic authenticated traffic. Debugger-injected protection failure followed by no-fault retry and actual Android VPN Settings service destruction followed by fresh activation are proved separately, with restoration. The forced return proves the error path; it does not prove actual Android OS denial or timing. Phone interruption/cancellation, concurrent replacement/destruction, physical wake/handover, IPv6 traffic and packet acceptance remain open until executed; host/JVM race controls retain their own scope.

### Expected-session recovery primitive controls — #73

The first source slice has no automatic trigger/controller. Exercise actual `Device.Recover` through settled exhaustion, duplicate/coalesced tokens, a fresh cycle followed by delayed old evidence, same-key peer removal/re-addition, current-endpoint mismatch and busy IPC/peer ownership. Inspect cleanup at publication; callbacks must not hold the peer recovery owner. Preserve pending-cycle eligibility through a throttled ordinary reset while accepted progress followed by stale expiry produces no eligible token. Accept a real protocol response and pause before completion-timer reset; the old retry callback cannot mint a claimable token. Cover responder key confirmation independently.

Prove static identity/recovery ordering under a pending static writer without recursive read-lock acquisition, and cancellation while initiation waits for static identity. Actual blocked UDP send must be cancelled before the native network write-lock wait; pair `Recover` with DOWN/destruction and retained handle/TUN lifetime. Cover ready-and-started, throttle/progress/cancellation skip, preparation failure, started/local-send failure and failed protection/open without initiation. Shared peers, endpoints, keys/settings, explicit/automatic ListenPort and warm-up intent remain preserved.

Compare mixed-peer UAPI snapshots by complete public-key-associated peer blocks: native `IpcGet` emits unsorted peer-map iteration. Preserve interface fields and every peer association; ignore only the transmitted-byte counter expected to change during initiation. Positive controls accept peer-block permutation and that counter change. Negative controls reject reassigned endpoints/PSKs/AllowedIPs, removed peers, changed device port and received-byte changes. Do not sort all lines together, which could hide cross-peer configuration changes.

Invoke actual `GoBackend.recover` with expected old session across same-model reconnect before JNI lookup. Preserve admitted evidence on obsolete completion, unavailable-context evidence without a throwable, original exception/Error identity and pre-entry/post-settlement interruption. JNI must carry the exact opaque episode and inspect the actual caller's interrupt flag without clearing it or poisoning the retained context. `transportReady()` requires current reusable ownership and never asserts authentication/reachability. Run host race/JNI, focused lifecycle/statistics/handshake/model controls, full unit/static gates and native ABI compilation; record actual results and remaining runtime limits in the PR.

The [2026-10-13 integrated Android record](reviews/2026-10-13-bounded-recovery-acceptance.md) adds independently verified explicit/omitted-port natural EXHAUSTED → controller → backend/JNI recovery, protected socket replacement with retained TUN/session/port, same-phone standard-kernel authenticated traffic and separately replayed sampled cleanup. A corrected V6 checker adjudicates unchanged explicit V5 execution; it does not relabel the original run. Fresh actual V6 omitted-port execution retains port 38795 and has two initiations without warm-up; its own artifact/checker/settlement/cleanup review passes. Both cleanup receipts report all 12 comparisons true. The native primitive's scoped Android core gate is met; the finite test-only entry hold establishes no natural latency. A separate fresh explicit-port budget run passes three natural exhaustion episodes, two automatic backend/JNI entries and third-attempt refusal, with four live TUN identity samples and independently replayed sampled restoration. This meets the scoped two-attempt controller budget gate without proving continuous TUN retention or authenticated traffic in that silent-endpoint run. Exact early-trigger cooldown rejection, BUSY deferral/four-entry bounds, literal zero, contention injection, cancellation/destruction, handover, Doze and broader paths retain separate controls. Test socket collision during `Open` as transport failure without initiation or port fallback; distinguish it from best-effort warm-up I/O failure on an already usable socket.

### Proposed recovery controls — #73

These are **acceptance requirements for the selected application controller**, not a complete passing report or shipping-delivery claim. The maintainer selected the [bounded pilot/defaults](backend-contracts.md#proposed-bounded-automatic-recovery--73) on 2026-10-12; the expected-session primitive is delivered through [PR #162](https://github.com/novakin/vpn0wn-android/pull/162). This manager/model controller slice implements finite admission/accounting and recovery snapshots; [PR #164](https://github.com/novakin/vpn0wn-android/pull/164) owns final controller-head proof and integration delivery. `TunnelRecoveryTest` drives the actual manager and final GoBackend with native-only responses, controlled elapsed/coroutine time and accepted service callbacks. The scoped integrated runs above do not replace final controller-head proof or the remaining controls below. Existing observation, presenter and manual-rebind evidence retain their original scopes.

The real-IO fixture uses bounded synchronous waiters that pump Main/current tasks without suspending; `runTest` must not consume the BUSY delay while returning an IO continuation. Advance coroutine time explicitly for the 999/1,000-ms checks, independently from Android elapsed time used by the 29,999/30,000-ms charged-spacing checks. Release and join every owned worker before resetting Main.

`knownNativeFailureSurvivesManualCancellationBeforeMainContinuation` must keep Main available until native entry. Startup's independently launched state reconciliation can hold the manager mutation lock across an IO-to-Main continuation; one `pump()` does not guarantee that continuation has arrived. A raw latch wait on Main can therefore prevent recovery admission. The control queues a Main-owned lock release after emitting exhaustion and uses the existing bounded pumping waiter until native entry. After releasing the native result, retain `awaitWithoutPumping` so manual cancellation still precedes the Main settlement continuation. Keep the five-second bounds, transport-failure accounting and session/startup assertions unchanged. PR #222 retains the original CI entry timeout and the controlled old-wait failure separately: the CI report did not capture its historical lock/thread state. This repairs test scheduling, not production recovery behavior.

- Through actual native timer/protocol owners, prove settled exhaustion cleanup and atomic episode claim. Exercise duplicate/coalesced/old events, exhausted A followed by fresh cycle B then delayed A, and authentication before/after admission. IPC locking alone must not pass the race control. Cover native throttle/no-initiation, empty staged packets, cleanup versus fresh initiation and truthful started/skipped/failed evidence.
- With deterministic time/responses, prove two conservatively charged slots per activation, one in flight, 30-second spacing with early-trigger drop, at most four backend requests, and one 1-second cancellable deferral only after positively non-mutating busy refusal. Refund only proven non-mutation without advancing spacing; keep confirmed/charged/unknown counts distinct. Exceptional or interrupted requests that may mutate cannot permit a third replacement. Ambiguous/unusable-context settlement must inhibit every later automatic request in that activation.
- Invoke actual expected-session backend admission with old work queued across same-model reconnect, service/backend/handle replacement and obsolete completion. Exercise pre-entry and post-admission cancellation and preserve interruption/exception/Error semantics. Run actual manager DOWN against blocked-send recovery; JNI must remain outside both `operationLock` and `stateLock`, and teardown must settle without a join/lock cycle. Include config/name/delete, tile/remote/manual classification, unchanged boot/system restoration and service destruction.
- Use a mixed failing eligible peer, healthy peer and peer without a current runtime endpoint. Preserve every saved profile, peer destination/setting/key, explicit/automatic ListenPort warm-up intent, TUN/device/session and protected-before-send boundary. A saved endpoint-free peer may qualify after natively learning a numeric runtime endpoint; prove admission without writing a saved Endpoint. Only the trigger peer receives an application initiation request; expose shared transport failure without inventing authentication failures. Missing numeric runtime endpoint/attribution and kernel mode admit no automatic mutation; preserve existing manual rebind callers.
- Cover failed protection/open, reusable versus terminal/unknown native context, retained unavailable transport and no implicit UP. Verify null/unknown/no-peer/Doze/rate-limit/budget/outcome meanings and diagnostic clearing on retirement. Historical authentication cannot hide known shared-transport failure. Do not infer causation, socket-generation authentication, Internet/DNS health or counts from coalesced events; retain only fixed safe diagnostics. Add no polling, ping, wake queue/lock, outside-tunnel DNS or endpoint/family rewrite.

Use existing JVM/Robolectric and native race/JNI seams for these controls, then zero-issue Detekt and actual final-head Android CI. Include a pending burst while the actual manager mutex is held, cancellation before backend entry, after explicit result capture and during the BUSY delay, a BUSY first entry followed by exceptional second entry, and no-op manual UP preserving same-session charges/shared failure. The request slot must remain occupied until native work joins and Main accounting completes. Require independent ultra review of final code and owning docs. The separate [activation-readout source](#detail-activation-recovery-readout-controls--73) now consumes the snapshot; its UI roadmap and supported-size/theme/font/RTL/accessibility proof remain distinct from controller acceptance.

Separately authorized rooted Android tests must cover successful/failed recovery, cancellation/destruction, real underlying-network handover and forced Doze, with traffic/packet observations appropriate to each claim and restored original state. Existing manual-rebind/presenter runs do not prove this controller. Kernel recovery, physical suspend, actual OS protection denial, hostname/family fallback and Internet/leak claims remain open until their specific selected controls run. A numeric-only pilot cannot close full #73; passing host tests does not select API/default policy.

- Provisioning JSON parsing:
  - `detect`, `plan`, `install`, `sync-status`, `add-peer`, `remove-peer`, `revoke-peer`, `backup`, `uninstall`
  - invalid JSON
  - `ok:false` error payloads
- Provisioning command failures:
  - exit `0`
  - nonzero with stderr
  - nonzero with stdout present and no stderr; payload-free failure summary
  - nonzero with empty output
- Remote shell execution boundaries:
  - command timeout when SSH connects but the process never exits
  - coroutine cancellation closes the remote command/session
  - unreachable server maps to a typed provisioning failure, not a generic crash
- Provisioning request args:
  - `VpsInstallRequest.installArgs()`
  - optional `--force`
  - `VpsAddPeerRequest.args()` with and without DNS
  - add shell-quoting regressions at the command-construction boundary; current request tests cover argument lists only
- Store behavior:
  - empty store
  - upsert insert
  - upsert replace by ID
  - remove one record
  - update status
  - corrupt JSON fallback
  - concurrent `upsert`, `remove`, and `updateStatus` operations preserve DataStore transaction semantics
- Formatters:
  - byte thresholds
  - compact handshake age
  - short key truncation
- Tunnel state helpers:
  - inactive
  - connecting
  - active with fresh handshake
  - no signal with stale handshake

  The bounded diagnostic follow-up adds a failure-only `TestWatcher` to `alreadyAddedResultRetainsMembershipAcrossReopening`. A temporary deliberate assertion failure at the persistence wait retained its original error type/message and exposed `Quick tile membership phase: persisted membership` in test XML. ### Connecting motion controls — #174

Run `HomeSelectionTest`, `TunnelPresentationTest` and `BindingAdaptersTest` alongside Detekt. The mixed-delete feedback fixture pauses Robolectric's synthetic Choreographer only after deletion settles and restores its prior state afterward; automatic frame advancement would otherwise run the longer connecting loops past the real Snackbar timeout before its exact message assertion. The fixture retains active rollback, surviving selection, retry availability and feedback text checks; this host clock control provides no native motion acceptance.

On an authorized isolated installation, record at least three complete CONNECTING loops in both Home and Detail. Require the existing warning color and halfway path, a 1.8-second cycle, smooth fades over the first and last 20%, and no visible reset flash. Check transitions into CONNECTING, connected blue motion, DOWN and ERROR without stale dots or colors. Reopen the view and switch between Home and Detail to confirm the current state and resumed motion.

Inspect native output at supported portrait and landscape sizes under system night and day requests, at normal and large text, with LTR and RTL layouts; record actual dimensions. The app retains its fixed navy palette under both system modes. Record effective animator scale 1 and 0: scale 1 must show the sweep; scale 0 must retain an opaque stationary warning dot at 25% of the full rail. Preserve and restore original animation, theme, text and locale settings, personal profiles and VPN state. `BindingAdaptersTest` and `TunnelPresentationTest` check presentation semantics, not motion frames. Link actual recordings and restoration evidence from the PR acceptance; [#47](https://github.com/novakin/vpn0wn-android/issues/47) retains broader scheduling/battery policy.

Before capture, bound actual native orientation and full diagram framing. Require owned foreground, Home with Detail Back absent, then an unchecked/enabled switch and native DOWN before exactly one UP tap and owned native admission. After any owned consent, bound the admission wait and stop on timeout without repeated mutations or a longer fixed sleep. Use the current Home app bar or native Detail scroll owner for a necessary bounded drag. Duplicate targets may resolve one physical point only when every attribute except positional index matches; differing bounds, state, package or other attributes must reject. Sample native WindowManager mode in the original baseline and verify its restoration separately from raw rotation settings. Preserve failed attempts and their raw receipts.

`TunnelDetailLifecycleTest` covers current config notifications without another load, selection/reset and stale/unrelated/destroyed-view config/state callbacks, alongside existing reversed-load and statistics controls. Separately authorized installed checks must compare diagram and Interface values, inspect selection/config refresh and relevant theme/rotation/font-scale output, and restore original profiles/settings/VPN state. Peerless UP proves activation/rendering only; #52 still owns broader device/accessibility acceptance.

### Android brand artwork controls

`BrandAssetsTest` uses native Android graphics for adaptive foreground/monochrome alpha, transparent counters, the central 66dp safe circle, gradient, default adaptive masks on API 33/36, tile padding/white mask, unmirrored RTL artwork and TV wordmark aspect. Native `BitmapFactory` checks every legacy density PNG's dimensions, shape and navy fill, and the 320×180 TV banner. Separate API 24/25 legacy-graphics controls resolve actual bitmap launcher resources at all five density qualifiers and check their native dimensions. They do not establish legacy device/raster output; default adaptive-mask previews do not cover every OEM mask.

`BindingAdaptersTest` checks the real outlined `ImageSpan`, preserved localized count and accessible product name. `HomeSelectionTest` renders empty and populated Home in six theme/size/2× text/RTL cases; existing Settings, tile and license tests cover unchanged navigation/state and all 28 complete notice nodes including Sora. Inspect actual previews for clipping, padding, contrast and preserved UI text roles. Authorized APK checks must separately inspect native normal/themed launcher, tile, Home, Settings/credits and TV output where available; disclose absent legacy/TV launcher/physical/TalkBack environments. Full #71 package migration and distribution acceptance remain separate.

For selectable credits, test keyboard focus outside touch mode, an observable focus indicator, unchanged rendered text color and complete selectable accessibility text. Compare focused/unfocused native platform output: Robolectric does not reproduce the platform's default foreground dimming. Inspect 4dp outline clearance and wrapping in both themes, short landscape and 2× RTL text; do not count a passing source property check as contrast proof. Capture settled output and retain rejected splash/rotation frames separately.

For the Home header, require the complete localized count to occupy one line without ellipsis at normal and 2× text, with wrapping allowed between artwork and count. Same-line wordmark/count ink centers must differ by no more than 1dp at both text sizes. Measure the complete white/cyan logo, including the full gradient slash, rather than its canvas or white lettering alone. Compare visible logo and Settings glyph gutters in LTR and RTL while retaining separate 48dp targets. In 320dp portrait at normal text, inspect at least 8dp guidance clearance above Add after the 112dp empty-artwork adjustment; short/large-text cases must keep full guidance and Add reachable through scrolling.

### Home reference source checks

[Current #80 acceptance](reviews/2026-10-18-presentation-translation-acceptance.md#selected-home-reference--80) composes the existing source controls with accepted native/output records. This remains the regression runbook; the linked criterion record owns the bounded completion interpretation and separates #36/#52 work.

The #80 Home source controls add cached endpoint-host semantics, latest/missing/future handshake ages and English/French wording in `BindingAdaptersTest`. `HomeSelectionTest` adds existing Add/Logs/Settings navigation, disabled Search, non-overlapping 48dp action targets and normal header/status/footer content checks to its six native preview configurations. For empty Home, center the standalone artwork and guidance as one group in the visible space between the header/status strip and fixed Add action, rather than the offscreen extent reserved for header collapse. Check settled startup before any header gesture, binding after initial header layout, and expanded, intermediate and collapsed header positions when the group fits; compare its vertical center with the clipped visible viewport within 1dp and keep the artwork horizontally centered. The [2026-10-12 caption refinement](ui-redesign-roadmap.md#empty-home-guidance-refinement--2026-10-12) uses 14sp Inter Tight, a 280dp maximum text measure, balanced wrapping and centered lines; verify native font scaling, complete localized copy and the existing instruction contrast. Actual Android output must cover normal portrait, short landscape, supported themes and 2× RTL text. When content exceeds the available height, scrolling must reveal the complete guidance above Add without changing artwork size, its decorative accessibility role, focus treatment or Add reachability. Native-graphics checks do not replace that runtime proof.

For empty Home keyboard focus, `HomeSelectionTest` renders the actual `home_empty_scroll` owner with focus on Add, then on the ScrollView, then back on Add. Six API 34 native-graphics configurations require unchanged nonzero instruction-color pixels and descendant geometry, a visible cyan focus outline, enabled readable instructions and a reachable Add action. The separate API 24 control checks inflation, focus ownership and Add-sheet navigation without claiming native pixel proof. Platform default highlighting stays enabled; the local focus-aware background replaces its overlay only for this container. Robolectric does not reproduce the platform's dimming overlay; its native failure control proves the missing visible focus outline, while paired physical output must establish the real contrast change. Before merge, separately authorized physical baseline and candidate captures must each pair ScrollView focus with Add focus at the same unchanged device configuration, checking instruction/background colors, visible keyboard focus and Add reachability. Supported size/theme/large-text/RTL output remains a separate actual-Android matrix on an AVD or phone under the existing UI acceptance requirements. Preserve original profiles and restore changed settings. [The 2026-10-11 scoped acceptance](reviews/2026-10-11-home-focus-acceptance.md) records the executed physical pairs, seven actual-Android focus cases, retained stopped setups and checked restoration; collapsed-header pairs do not establish expanded-header matrix acceptance. #80 remains open for its broader acceptance.

The later [Android brand adoption](#android-brand-artwork-controls) supersedes the earlier Inter Tight product-wordmark rendering with approved outlined artwork. For the [2026-10-10 screenshot correction](ui-redesign-roadmap.md#home-detail-correction--2026-10-10), retain accessible `vpn0wn`, Inter Tight labels/cipher, and regular 13sp JetBrains Mono endpoint value. Check the end-aligned endpoint block and decorative external-link icon do not overlap, clip the value or become an unintended action. Preserve cached configured-host semantics without DNS or public-IP lookup. Exercise existing cipher selection with and without PSK; render that value once beside status and keep it absent beneath LAST HANDSHAKE. Inspect both themes, narrow portrait, 2× short landscape and RTL, including unavailable endpoint/handshake and long-hostname cases. The linked correction record documents the installed six-case visual scope and its separately retained physical edge-case/TalkBack limits. Current exact-head checks and delivery state belong to [PR #108](https://github.com/novakin/vpn0wn-android/pull/108); prior Home evidence retains its original scope.

### Security Regression Tests

Editor mask controls also require a real `PasswordTransformationMethod` and transformed text after initial/delayed loads and policy/selection resets; comparing numeric input types alone cannot establish masking. A separate reveal/revocation control verifies both key fields retain their custom typefaces when Android changes password variations.

`ZipExporterPolicyTest` and `TunnelEditorKeyPolicyTest` drive the existing preference/fragment owners with synthetic restriction/authentication callbacks. They cover denial before click/focus, revocation during authentication/config loading and at stream acquisition, allowed ZIP contents/no-lock paths, terminal failure/cancellation, nonterminal failed samples, duplicate callbacks and stale editor/Settings views or selections. Assert actual password transformation and transformed text as well as numeric input flags; reveal/remask must preserve each field's typeface. `BiometricAuthenticatorTest` separately models AndroidX's Activity-shared callback slot, overlapping fragments, queued events, retained rotation, terminal/duplicate delivery, detached/saved owners and failed launch cleanup. These are Robolectric policy/ownership controls, not Android DPC or biometric proof. Real #166 timing acceptance, when separately authorized, needs a disposable managed user/profile with denied/allowed pairs; do not enroll or change ownership on the personal phone. [The scoped record](reviews/2026-10-12-app-root-security-audit.md) owns actual native DPC/PIN results and instrumentation limits. Blocking writes and already-visible/copied data retain the [contract limits](backend-contracts.md#import-ordering-and-export).

- Passwords are not included in snackbars, logs, exceptions, or copied text.
- Generic copy handlers do not accidentally expose private keys; deliberate authorized profile export/import retains the full required credentials.
- Password/session secret is cleared on cancel and view destruction.
- Authenticated transport always requires the accepted canonical pin; trust flags cannot bypass it. The [confirmed host-trust controls](#confirmed-ssh-host-trust-and-field-presentation--143) must verify pre-authentication review, durable trust and changed-key refusal.
- Uninstall, revoke, remove peer, and delete tunnel require confirmation.

### Manual Smoke Tests

Required before merging UI or backend provisioning work, except the explicitly authorized source-only recovery integration in G1 of the [active goal](improvement-plan.md#active-enhancement-and-performance-goal). That narrow exception requires meaningful local regression checks, native content inspection for affected UI and independent ultra review; physical/device-provider acceptance stays open and must pass in G4 before delivery or goal completion. It does not waive live provisioning acceptance or authorize APK/device operations.

- Import a WireGuard config.
- Toggle tunnel up/down.
- Visit detail while live stats update.
- Open logs, filter logs, share logs.
- Rotate device on home/detail/editor/VPS wizard.
- Background and foreground the app while a tunnel is up.
- Delete a tunnel.
- Exercise Android VPN permission flow on a clean install.

### Consent fixture handoff repair — 2026-10-16

[PR #204](https://github.com/novakin/vpn0wn-android/pull/204) first failed [CI run 38954896722](https://github.com/novakin/vpn0wn-android/actions/runs/38954896722) at `b2bb4d11`: 917 UI tests completed, one failure in `recreatedConsentPreservesRequestedDownDirection`, with later release/native checks unexecuted. The assertion observed DOWN instead of UP inside `launchConsent`, before result delivery. `ControlledBackend.setState` queues `TunnelManager.onTunnelStateChange`; its application-owned callback can read the reflected replacement inactive backend before publishing state. This is a synthetic handoff race, not new evidence of a production consent failure.

The test now joins only new application children from the synthetic UP transition before replacing the backend, retaining pre-existing DataStore jobs and every assertion. At `b2bb4d11` plus this four-line test delta, focused `VpnConsentLifecycleTest` passes all 13 API-28 controls without failures/errors/skips, and Detekt has zero findings in WSL/OpenJDK 21. Independent ultra review confirms the callback-lifetime mechanism and bounded drain. Final committed-head CI remains required; this JVM repair adds no native/device or #177 completion proof.
