The artwork generator and tracked launcher-background alias must agree. Preserve selector order, geometry, colors, dimensions, fonts, localized text, preference/persistence keys and current `vpnown_*` artwork. Investigate new raster differences; known unchanged-source wordmark-edge variation is a renderer limit, not a claim that every capture matches. This mechanical slice requires no new native ABI/APK/device run and does not complete #71; localized and native private naming have distinct mapped checks in this section, while artifact/device delivery remains separate.

The [2026-10-11 scoped record](reviews/2026-10-11-namespace-acceptance.md) records 483 debug JVM tests, zero Detekt findings, the targeted release startup control, six host race groups/seven JNI controls and debug four-ABI artifact checks. Rooted Pixel proof adds new-ID startup/consent, synthetic native-provider import, same-phone authenticated HTTP, one manual rebind with two successful protection callbacks and sequential DOWN/reconnect. Restoration passes all 12 preservation fields with zero mismatches and owned resources/new package absent. Missing bracketing descriptors/generations and timed-out destruction observers preserve retained-TUN/service-destruction gaps; the Python-generated archive establishes no legacy-exporter cross-app migration.

The [identity contract](backend-contracts.md#application-and-source-identity--71) describes implemented source identities and retained compatibility names. Source implementation is separate from executed proof. Run both repository debug JVM suites, targeted release startup and zero-issue Detekt using current selectors; compare executed class/test inventory with pre-migration reports so package moves cannot silently reduce coverage. Include `ApplicationIdentityTest`, `ToolsInstallerAdmissionTest`, tile, backend lifecycle, transfer/persistence and affected Settings controls. Require canonical SET actions and the sole legacy REFRESH alias, unchanged receiver permission/remote-control preference gates, independent provider/tile/admin targets, and exact selected-ID admission by `ToolsInstaller.install()` and rejection of unsupported install calls before those calls extract tools or enter root commands.

For localized resource naming, compare complete source bytes with only the 214 `tunnl_*` to `vpn0wn_*` key substitutions and removal of the two unused default/French subtitle declarations admitted. Preserve every other translation, placeholder, quantity, qualifier, escape and missing-locale fallback. Require zero old keys in current source/resource/test consumers and compile the generated R/DataBinding consumers. Bracket the rename with the same `BindingAdaptersTest`, `AddPeerPresentationTest`, `HomeSelectionTest`, `PeerHandshakePresentationTest` and `TunnelEditorHeaderTest` selectors plus zero-issue Detekt; retain raw baseline XML/PNGs before the candidate run and compare/inspect the existing Home, Detail/peer, Add and editor size/theme/font/RTL controls. This resource-only slice needs no Go/C/JNI/ABI build or APK/device operation; source/native-render preservation does not extend installed-artifact acceptance.

Compile debug and non-debug variants without unnecessary APK assembly. Generate debug/release/googleplay merged manifests and require `com.vpn0wn.android.debug`/`com.vpn0wn.android`, renamed components, variant permission/provider authorities and no upstream updater. Inspect generated DataBinding/R/BuildConfig references and the fork POM's group/version/metadata. Run affected host Go race/JNI/overlay controls, build all four Android ABIs and inspect the actual five `Java_com_vpn0wn_android_backend_GoBackend_` exports. Verify incremental output/symbol inventory and variant-native private UAPI package paths while preserving the `wireguard` cache leaf and library/CLI names. These checks are required acceptance, not claimed results in this guide.

After separate APK/device authorization, verify renamed startup, VPN authorization, activation/traffic/reconnect/teardown, retained-TUN/JNI callbacks, tile setup, boot/always-on, control permission behavior and old/new-app coexistence. Verify selected profiles/settings/fleet transfer and rollback with both installations preserved; inspect actual identity and complete license payload. Existing legacy-ID physical/AVD/native results do not prove renamed binaries. Optional root/kernel compatible-tool reuse, owned installation and privileged cleanup need their own authorized environment and #103 integration gate. No root write, server migration, release, DPI, battery or performance proof follows from this source rename. Current first-party source/operator names identify vpn0wn; remaining attribution/provenance and distributed-artifact obligations stay open under #71.

## Remaining G4 device acceptance

For localized UI checks, prefer unique package-owned resource IDs. When a row shares IDs and needs exact-text selection, derive expected labels from the reviewed source's locale resources and confirm them against native hierarchy output; do not assume every RTL label falls back to English. Include a source or retained-native control that rejects the wrong-language selector. Full hierarchy text and clipped accessibility bounds do not prove complete raster text: inspect the original pixels and recapture obscured or partly visible rows before visual sign-off.

The [combined Downloads record](reviews/2026-10-18-native-downloads-acceptance.md) meets #28's agreed source/native/physical boundary. The [current presentation/translation composition](reviews/2026-10-18-presentation-translation-acceptance.md) meets scoped #34/#35/#36/#80/#195 criteria; final documentation review, CI and actual delivery/issue readback remain gates. A different independent runtime reviewer accepts the fresh [#36 native keyboard cohort](reviews/2026-10-18-presentation-translation-acceptance.md#home-selection--36): actual focused checkbox/count/zero-Delete, standard AppBar cluster traversal, Select all, exact-name warning/Cancel, normal exit and sampled restoration. Frame 18 owns settled confirmation output; frame 17 retains state/transition credit. Earlier source metadata, ordinary taps and empty-Home focus are not substituted for that cohort. Pre-confirmation Cancel adds no native admitted-deletion cancellation result. Historical completion notes and the reusable G4 steps below retain their original environments and limits; they do not add whole-app #52, acoustic speech, physical lens or all-state/OEM requirements to these narrower issues. [The physical import record](reviews/2026-10-08-g4-import-device-acceptance.md) and [isolated native follow-up](reviews/2026-10-09-g4-private-native-acceptance.md) own their actual completed checks. Reuse endpoint-free synthetic configs and fresh unique names; standard QR import asks for a name, while file imports use filenames.

The [2026-10-11 rooted Settings follow-up](reviews/2026-10-11-rooted-settings-acceptance.md) adds settled English portrait output at 1× text under system day/night requests on the retained reviewed APK. Restore on boot's changed DataStore bytes were observed before actual landscape recreation and reopening; returning its original value restored byte-identical preferences. Logs/Back navigation and all 12 fresh preservation fields, including app-UID Magisk policy, passed. Four rejected matrix setup attempts supply no additional visual acceptance; physical large-text/RTL cases remain unproved. This is one persisted-switch control, not actual boot or privileged-action proof. Other switches, tile/authentication/export/provider actions, administrator conditions, TalkBack and the remaining supported matrix keep #34/#52 open; the report owns artifact provenance, failed attempts and restoration limits.

The separate [2026-10-11 API 36 Settings matrix](reviews/2026-10-11-api36-settings-output-acceptance.md) adds nine admitted English/Arabic configurations, including 2× text and actual landscape, on the reviewed source-equivalent APK. Native top/bottom output, scrolling and header Back were inspected; all 13 recorded baseline fields matched after restoration and the owned emulator was stopped. Day/night are system-mode requests, and the app keeps its fixed dark palette. English fallback strings, scroll-viewport limits, failed attempts and provenance remain explicit. This supplies isolated output/navigation proof, not physical enlarged/RTL, preference mutation, privileged actions or spoken TalkBack acceptance.

The [2026-10-14 rooted Settings record](reviews/2026-10-14-settings-device-acceptance.md) adds a remote-control preference round-trip through actual rotation/reopening, byte-identical preference restoration and native tile Cancel/Add/owned removal. Independent original-pixel/raw records establish 6/6 selected physical visual configurations across distinct matching sessions after the three narrow RTL gaps were resolved. The latest paired and singleton restoration each pass composite 15/15, owner 14/14, seven extra fields and exact original timeout readback within sampled scope. The owning record keeps failed attempts, the prior paired portrait toast HOLD and separately timed preservation distinct. Conditional privileged/admin/API, spoken TalkBack and action gates remain open under #34/#52.

The [2026-10-18 current-artifact attempt](reviews/2026-10-14-settings-device-acceptance.md#current-artifact-talkback-attempt--2026-10-18) is PARTIAL: native generated feedback was observed, but ADB/UHID did not establish app TalkBack traversal/activation. Its exact sampled restoration, retained native preference migration and unbound prior permission flags are recorded separately; this accessibility step and #34/#35/#36/#52 remain open.

1. Establish a new exclusive interval and fresh state: package/APK provenance, current personal tunnel intent, config/preferences hashes, draft/screen, device settings with presence/absence, locale, current tile inventory and any existing export output. Preserve unrelated data and accessibility services. The expired interval and its restoration snapshot cannot authorize or initialize a later run.
2. Repeat reversible Settings switch/rotation/reopen/Back/log actions, then restore the observed original value. Inspect the remaining insets/large-text/RTL cases without counting earlier output as new proof. Record actual eligible groups; API/admin/kernel rows unavailable on this device remain explicitly conditional.
3. Scan the known synthetic QR through the physical camera and save a uniquely named DOWN profile. A second screen or print supplies the target; image import does not establish lens decoding. Revoking an already granted camera permission can kill the process that owns the userspace VPN: denial/recovery belongs in an isolated synthetic environment. Do not manufacture it on the working personal-UP package.
4. Check physical peer RX/TX copy actions against the captured displayed strings without copying keys. Inspect missing/zero rows on a synthetic DOWN peer and return to the original selection. Clipboard action proof does not establish equality with an independent native counter snapshot; retain that separate boundary.
5. Capture settled selected/unselected/zero Home states and check recreation/count/identity and confirmation cancellation. Delete only the exact session-owned names shown in confirmation. Existing `HomeSelectionTest` and `TunnelTestEnvironment` provide deterministic insertion/rename, blocked admission, partial failure and cancellation controls; do not modify personal files or activate a synthetic userspace tunnel to manufacture failure on the phone.
6. Exercise actual TalkBack traversal, button/switch/checkbox actions, selected counts, labels and announcements with coordinated spoken-output confirmation. Preserve and restore existing accessibility services/settings. Installed service, hierarchy labels and JVM semantics alone do not prove speech.
7. Keep authentication Cancel separate from export-operation cancellation. [Native API 36 Cancel/success checks](reviews/2026-10-09-g4-private-native-acceptance.md#api-36-credential-authentication-follow-up) cover absent and existing outputs; applicable physical authentication remains a separate gate. Current `ZipExporterPreference` exports every saved tunnel to fixed `vpn0wn-export.zip` with overwrite enabled; it has no destination picker or selected-profile export. The filename rename does not migrate or clean up older `wireguard-export.zip` copies. Real provider failure/write/cancellation tests need isolated synthetic data where necessary. Reuse `DownloadsFileSaverTest` and `ZipExporterPreferenceTest` for deterministic source controls; their provider fixtures and manual output deletion are not physical production-cleanup proof. Keep [native API 28 permission/filesystem checks](reviews/2026-10-09-g4-private-native-acceptance.md#api-28-legacy-storage-follow-up) distinct from API 29+ MediaStore results.
8. Restore only session-owned changes and the intended current tunnel state. Verify fresh app-file comparisons, settings/locale, removal of hash-confirmed owned uploads/outputs and MediaStore rows, stopped commands and phone release. Never restore a stale archive over live data. Record actual results, unavailable cases and issue-state readback before claiming complete acceptance.

An isolated AVD may exercise native framework/provider/permission callbacks with the unchanged APK and synthetic data. Set up only its owned configuration; target its explicit serial, preserve the existing ADB server and never issue unqualified ADB mutations. Camera emulation and host-powered execution do not establish physical camera, Pixel cutout/gesture, root/kernel, TalkBack speech or battery behavior. An API 36 result supplies no pre-29 permission/filesystem proof; matching API 28 checks below retain their own scope. Native and physical results remain separately scoped; obtain applicable missing proof or an explicit scope decision before issue closure.

The [2026-10-09 private AVD runs](reviews/2026-10-09-g4-private-native-acceptance.md) establish bounded native camera-permission retry, tile Cancel/Add, repeated switch/recreation, canonical export/MediaStore overwrite and settled Home selection/cancellation proof. Separate API 28 checks add storage-permission denial/retry and filesystem success/overwrite; API 36 credential checks add authentication Cancel/success and preservation of existing output after Cancel. The [API 28 export open-failure follow-up](reviews/2026-10-09-g4-private-native-acceptance.md#api-28-export-open-failure-follow-up) adds actual pre-ownership `FileOutputStream` acquisition failure: `FileNotFoundException` (`Is a directory`) surfaced, the preexisting directory/sentinel and stored configs/preferences survived, the export action was enabled again, and retry produced the canonical two-config ZIP. The later [#84 repaired-APK session](reviews/2026-10-09-g4-private-native-acceptance.md#camera-repair-acceptance--2026-10-09) proves corrected native denial/exit/retry advice; it does not complete physical QR or broader Add/peer acceptance. The [#85 repaired-APK session](reviews/2026-10-09-g4-private-native-acceptance.md#tile-membership-repair-acceptance--2026-10-09) verifies retained tile membership and row eligibility across native actions, independent callbacks, Settings/rotation and process reopening. Those sessions left export-operation cancellation, acquired legacy cleanup and MediaStore failure acceptance incomplete; the [combined Downloads record](reviews/2026-10-18-native-downloads-acceptance.md) now meets #28's boundary. Successful export/manual output removal alone did not supply cleanup proof. Other physical gates above, real QR camera decoding, tile VPN toggling, admitted deletion cancellation and broader active-state recovery remain unproved. Secure-prompt hierarchy/callback proof does not establish rendered credential-screen fidelity. #52 remains open; the follow-up record owns session restoration evidence separately from these action checks.

The [API 28 admitted-delete failure follow-up](reviews/2026-10-09-g4-private-native-acceptance.md#api-28-admitted-delete-failure-follow-up) proves a narrower native failure boundary: app-UID directory write denial made both confirmed synthetic DOWN deletions fail, while configs, settings-file absence, selection/counts and retry survived. Restoring the exact original mode allowed confirmed deletion and empty Home. Use only an owned isolated environment for such permission changes; preserve current path/mode/UID and persistent-file evidence, restore mode before retry/uninstall and verify cleanup/termination. This does not replace partial-success, admitted cancellation, active-state recovery or physical acceptance; #36/#52 stay open.

The [2026-10-11 active-delete follow-up](reviews/2026-10-11-home-active-delete-acceptance.md) adds real native config deletion failure on one synthetic UP userspace tunnel, same-operation UP recovery with selected survivor/authenticated private HTTP, and successful retry after exact directory-mode restoration. The original helper failed on a transient duplicate-row hierarchy; later settled evidence reconciles that operation without repeating it. Failure feedback is recorded in XML/logs, not the earlier transitional PNG. Config preservation retains its assertion/journal limit and no preference-byte baseline was captured. Device/peer/AVD cleanup passed its recorded scope; retained userdata includes a cached VPN package/UID entry with inactive type `-1` outside the compared baseline. That active-delete run did not prove partial progress or admitted cancellation; other environments and physical/accessibility/provider gates keep #36/#52 open.

The [2026-10-11 partial-delete trial](reviews/2026-10-11-home-partial-delete-acceptance.md) proves one native API 36 DOWN-only partial-success case on a fresh isolated userdebug AVD. After a reversible non-config immutable preflight, alpha's actual store failure preceded bravo's successful deletion; Home showed one-of-two progress and kept alpha selected with retry enabled. Exact original reported flags, file identity and bytes were restored before one successful survivor retry and empty persisted inventory. Both preference directories remained absent. The PNG shows complete progress but clips the error tail; fresh XML/logs retain the full error. Cleanup's 16 baseline fields matched, effective adbd UID and SELinux mode were restored and the owned AVD stopped with shared processes preserved; `service.adb.root` changed from empty to `0`. Admitted cancellation, other environments and physical/TalkBack/provider gates remain open under #36/#52.

## Transfer-speed and battery validation

[G4/#52](https://github.com/novakin/vpn0wn-android/issues/52) tracks final validation for the [active enhancement/performance goal](improvement-plan.md#active-enhancement-and-performance-goal). The [initial G4 record](reviews/2026-10-07-g4-preflight-investigation.md) establishes authorized CI artifact/install proof and bounded incident observations; the [repeated network record](reviews/2026-10-07-g4-network-baselines.md) owns subsequent path measurements and their limits. The current composition above meets #34/#35/#36's scoped presentation criteria; broader G4 work still includes clean Wi-Fi context, controlled energy baselines and demonstrated optimization. Issues own the selected device/endpoint, primary metric, numeric target and guardrails after baseline measurement. APK assembly/install and a real benchmark target need their own explicit authorization. Start with Wi-Fi; mobile-data and root/kernel coverage are conditional on the authorized environment.

The [candidate-device follow-up](reviews/2026-10-07-g4-candidate-device-review.md) adds #55 warning confirmation and bounded Add/Home action/output proof on source `4e2f473d`. It preserves personal config/preferences and restores checked device settings; the changed Android runtime marker is identified separately. TalkBack, remaining peer/Settings/provider boundaries and complete physical acceptance stay open. The phone used LTE while charging; no speed/energy baseline was attempted.

The [2026-10-08 import/device follow-up](reviews/2026-10-08-g4-import-device-acceptance.md) uses the existing `f4a4b147` APK. It adds controlled mixed-ZIP/image-QR import, portrait keyboard clearance and synthetic-only Home deletion with exact app-file preservation/restoration. Earlier switch/tile/export observations have weaker ownership evidence; export cancellation remains unproved. At that checkpoint, camera QR/denial, TalkBack, peer copying/live counters, remaining Settings/provider callbacks and admitted-deletion failure/cancellation still needed their own proof. The native follow-ups above retain their separate scope. Transition captures do not establish settled selection fidelity or extend the earlier output matrix.

Record both baseline and candidate app revisions. Use the same phone, Android version, build type, test endpoint, network, backend and synthetic test profile for a paired comparison. Record signal/network quality, thermal state, charging state, foreground/screen state, MTU, routes, keepalive and background load. Keep private profiles/raw device reports local; commit only sanitized methodology and aggregates. Use existing Android profiling and benchmark tools rather than creating a performance framework.

Before a timed device measurement, establish one chat/runner as phone owner and coordinate other ADB clients/tools so they stay idle during the agreed interval. If concurrent use is discovered, stop the owned run, retain incomplete/diagnostic evidence and agree the handoff before restoration or another measurement.

Confirm whether the Wi-Fi router already uses VPN before calling app-off traffic direct. App-off/app-on measurements behind that router compare router-only and nested VPN paths; they are not clean direct Internet or upstream phone-handshake/DPI proof. For connectivity diagnosis, use an authorized cellular path with Wi-Fi disabled and the current default network verified, or an authorized Wi-Fi path without the router VPN. Record mobile-data approval/limit, establish the test source before restricting the temporary listener and verify the expected server-observed source in each successful exchange. Preserve historical raw filenames while correcting their interpretation in sanitized aggregates. Restore each session's fresh starting state and verify personal file hashes and temporary-device/server cleanup.

For foreground/battery comparisons, verify actual animation behavior and effective system scale before measuring; a stored setting alone is insufficient. Record whether each temporarily changed Android setting existed, and restore an absent setting by deletion rather than writing the string `null`. If the maintainer resumes use during cleanup and the desired final tunnel state is unclear, confirm it before further toggles. Distinguish temporary running-state preference changes from personal config/settings edits; report final preservation and any failed automatic restoration separately. Do not copy a stale archive over current data or classify a failed cleanup command as successful because its load trials passed or later recovery succeeded.

Verify the profile again after interruptions or user-reported recovery. A changed ListenPort invalidates a fixed-profile comparison; preserve the new value and separate the runs. `UDP preflight sent` and active Android VPN-service state do not prove endpoint delivery or sustained health. A failed/incomplete iperf run is failure evidence, not a throughput baseline. Separate capped diagnostic traffic from uncapped baseline scenarios and stop traffic when the maintainer reports the path blocked. Obtain a restored authorized path and confirm its state before resuming the repeated protocol.

The [G2 source investigation](reviews/2026-10-07-performance-source-investigation.md) proposes #47 as the single battery candidate and retains current transfer code until G4 proves a bottleneck. Include covered single-pane Home, visible two-pane Home/Detail and hidden attached animations in the baseline; resumed/attached state does not establish actual visibility. #48's shared-read/late-publication repair has separate source acceptance under [JVM unit tests](#jvm-unit-tests); it establishes no measured battery or transfer gain. The four original temporary characterization controls retain their historical scope; they are not device frame-frequency or energy/throughput evidence.

The [2026-10-08 presentation CPU record](reviews/2026-10-08-g4-ui-cpu-baselines.md) records the post-#48 baseline's verified APK provenance, three accepted Home/covered-Detail/Settings/background windows per scenario and separate scheduling traces. CPU and scheduling evidence from a charging phone remains diagnostic; controlled energy, targets and #47 implementation are separate gates. Preserve exact counter endpoints, process identity, elapsed time and current presentation proof when collecting new windows. Retain excluded/mislabeled raw windows and their reasons; count only the verified scenario, and keep instrumented traces separate from process-counter results. Compare a later candidate against a baseline with the same unrelated source changes; never attribute an earlier build's statistics-policy difference to #47.

The [unplugged energy pilots](reviews/2026-10-08-g4-energy-pilots.md) retain uncontrolled diagnostic endpoints under a different Wi-Fi/wireless-ADB context. Another controller's ADB use was confirmed during testing; its contribution is unquantified. A changed Detail viewport cannot be counted as a fixed-view repeat, and the deliberately interrupted first screen-off window has no endpoint result or product-failure verdict. Measurements stopped and authorized cleanup was verified through unlocked USB access: personal configuration/preferences match, test-app VPN, Wi-Fi and wireless debugging are OFF, automatic brightness is applied and timeout restored. The dynamic numeric brightness value was preserved; eleven of twelve final saved checked values match exactly. The record distinguishes a stalled cleanup command from later successful readback. Preserve raw observations/exclusions, record uncontrolled traffic and distinguish whole-device estimates from app energy. Boundary pairs do not establish counter resolution/cadence, exclusive use or a matched workload. These diagnostics complete no controlled baseline or target gate. For controlled repeats, verify viewport as well as activity identity and keep observed interruptions separate from inferred causes.

### Controlled #47 baseline preparation

Refresh the retained CPU/energy helpers into a new ignored session directory before execution. Their old transport, display values, network label and restoration snapshots belong to completed pilots. Supply the current agreed target/context and preserve exact CPU tick endpoints, process identity and separate monotonic CPU/battery intervals. This is a reuse of measurement tooling, not a new performance framework or an accepted baseline.

Begin with a separate counter-capability diagnostic: timestamp charge-counter readings at five-second intervals for an initial ten minutes, extending if changes are sparse. Report observed increments and plateau intervals under that instrumentation; they bound observed granularity/cadence, not true resolution or accuracy. Do not pool these sampled windows with the quiet energy repeats. If counter behavior cannot resolve scenario depletion and variability, improve the measurement/duration before freezing a target; CPU proxies alone cannot establish battery savings or a verified no-change result.

The provisional quiet order is Home → covered Detail → ordinary screen-off, then Detail → screen-off → Home, then screen-off → Home → Detail. Initial holds are 300 seconds for each foreground view and 600 seconds for screen-off: 60 minutes total, excluding capability checks, setup, thermal settling and restoration. Lengthen holds before accepting repeats if counter steps/noise are too large. Fix the Detail viewport, actual animation behavior/effective scales, brightness, profile, network/router context and background workload; boundary activity identity alone does not prove stable presentation or uninterrupted residence.

Physically unplug USB and require every reported power source OFF, discharging and no battery simulation. Wireless ADB/network conditions must match later comparisons; ordinary noninteractive screen-off with wireless debugging is not hardware deep-suspend proof. No transfer load is part of this idle/foreground baseline. Reject controlled acceptance for observed concurrent use, charging, interruptions or changed viewport/network/profile/process; retain excluded records and traffic/thermal context rather than discarding inconvenient depletion. Three baseline repeats precede separate paired candidate trials. Freeze the primary metric, numeric target and regression limits in #47 only after controlled spread is known; retain configuration/connectivity/rates/appearance/accessibility guardrails and the original-state restoration protocol above.

| Scenario | Measures and control |
| --- | --- |
| Transfer | Compare direct and VPN download/upload against the same authorized endpoint. Use fixed warm-up, duration and stream count; inspect sustained throughput, latency/loss and CPU cost. Run at least five paired A/B trials, varying A/B order to reduce thermal/time bias, and report median plus spread. Increase repetitions only when noise prevents a decision. A direct result is context, not an optimization comparison between app revisions. |
| Idle/screen-off | Compare equal-duration idle-connected windows with the same network/profile/keepalive. Run at least three paired windows long enough to resolve the chosen energy measure; record wakeups, CPU activity and available energy evidence. Android modeled estimates, charge-counter deltas and battery percentage have different limits. A charging/USB-powered session does not prove reduced battery drain. |
| Foreground UI | Profile list/detail polling, request overlap, drawing/animation, allocations and state refresh under equal conditions. Check hidden/stopped/foreground transitions. CPU/wakeup reductions are proxies; call them battery savings only when the corresponding controlled energy comparison establishes that result. |
| Sustained traffic | Compare energy for the same amount of transferred data and a fixed-rate workload; report duration, thermal behavior and throughput separately. Do not interpret a faster run's lower total energy as an idle-power reduction. |

Before selecting an optimization, repeat each baseline transfer scenario at least five times and each idle-energy scenario at least three times to establish variability. These baseline repeats precede the paired candidate trials above; they do not count as a comparison between app revisions. Before each optimization, freeze its primary target and allowed regressions in the issue based on measured baseline variability. Require repeated paired benefit beyond the observed noise and all declared guardrails. Inconclusive results require more focused evidence or a no-change decision. JVM formatting/lifecycle tests and a successful APK build do not establish speed or energy gains.

Preserve configuration parsing/persistence, activation/teardown and cancellation, DNS/routes, connectivity/NAT behavior, accurate statistics and UI/accessibility. MTU, endpoint or keepalive experiments must identify the specific path and restoration method; do not change global defaults from one phone/server result. Userspace is the baseline unless the authorized environment supports kernel testing. Keep device/native limitations and untested network/backend combinations explicit.

## Source-inspected Coverage

The phone presentation, Creator result and Snackbar tint suites are described under [JVM unit tests](#jvm-unit-tests); they are included in the inventory below.

The source inventory below includes merged #14/#13/#16/#15, the merged #22 suites, the phone follow-up, #33 log recovery, merged #34 Settings source, #35 Add/peer source, #36 Home source, #28 Downloads ownership, #55 debug-warning controls, the Settings structure-ID regression, shared statistics teardown controls from #68 and #48 model-statistics regressions. The shared TunnelTestEnvironment fixture is not a test suite. This is an inventory, not an execution total; historical counts retain their original reviewed revisions in `docs/reviews/`. Guard tests use fake execute calls for timeout, I/O failure and cancellation propagation; they do not prove an entered SSH command, upload timeout or actual channel/session closure. Robolectric also covers detail-fragment selection/destruction, kernel-preference failure recovery and bounded Settings/Add/peer/Home navigation/rendering; whole-app/device proof remains separate.

`TunnelManagerTest` covers admitted/queued transitions, deletion/config failure recovery, cancelled initialization, stale state snapshots/native notifications, restoration intent and backend-switch admission/rollback. `ApplicationBackendLifecycleTest` proves cancellation before initializer execution settles backend waiters. `TunnelDetailLifecycleTest` covers reversed/cleared config selections, delayed peer statistics after selection/destruction, disconnect clearing without waiting for an in-flight fetch and failure clearing. `KernelModuleEnablerPreferenceTest` checks failed shutdown restores its setting/control. `GoBackendLifecycleTest` covers prompt destruction while its lock is held, dead-service rejection/future replacement, cleanup identity/callback ordering and native callback liveness, originating-thread interruption, exact-service/descriptor protection and Error propagation, including state/DOWN/service settlement when native teardown rethrows an Error. Config serialization controls retain explicit/absent ports; native-owner packet/port tests are separate. Synthetic owner/native-call shadows exercise no successful native activation, real VPN traffic, process exit or device lifecycle. See [original lifecycle evidence](development.md#tunnel-lifecycle-and-transition-settlement--2026-10-06) and [peer follow-up](development.md#add-sheet-and-peer-presentation-recovery--2026-10-06).

The [#37 compatibility review](reviews/2026-10-07-udp-compatibility-review.md) retains the merged backend and rejects the preserved mandatory/random-port experiment. Its eight existing controls were rerun for that decision; bind/send exhaustion and DNS failure/sleep interruption remain source traced rather than injected. Native port/NAT handoff, successful activation, packet traffic and reconnect need separate physical proof. No speed/battery benefit is established by retaining source.

Historical baseline [#55 controls](reviews/2026-10-07-g4-settings-add-debug-review.md) observed preflight tag 0 at socket protection and restoration of tagged/untagged caller state after rejected protection and a successful one-byte loopback send. Settings records whether its kernel-support probe runs on Main and retains the existing preference eligibility controls. These JVM tests do not certify native socket accounting or candidate-APK StrictMode output. The same scoped record covers actual Settings/Add phone captures across portrait, landscape, system light-mode, 200% text and Arabic RTL, with unchanged app files and restored checked settings. Its physical acceptance limits retain their original scope; no repeated performance result is added.

The [Settings structure-ID regression in `SettingsActivityTest`](reviews/2026-10-07-settings-resource-id-review.md) binds and lays out every real preference adapter row on API 36, dispatches Android's framework assist capture and checks roots/descendants for invalid ID zero before and after recreation. Each adapter enables stable IDs and each bound holder retains its non-`NO_ID` item identity; identity equality across separate adapter instances is not asserted. Synthetic kernel support exposes the full applicable modern Settings rows. The owner fix changes no geometry, keys, values or action. Separate phone confirmation on Pixel 10a reproduced nine errors on the previous candidate; three new Settings opens and landscape recreation had zero after verified in-place replacement. Native Back/log navigation and settled portrait/landscape output were checked; personal config/preferences were unchanged and nine device values restored. Full switch/tile/export/accessibility behavior and the precise capture caller remain unverified; #34/#52 stay open. The explicit launch capture does not cover complete package-replacement startup or a newly established preflight path.

`FileConfigStoreTest` covers temporary local files, successful profile round trips, partial-write/native-rename failure preserving prior content, failed creates with no usable profile, conflicting names and concurrent create/save/delete ordering. The Context writer and native-call shadow inject failures without modifying user data. The reviewed suite does not prove Android filesystem durability, actual disk-full/power-loss behavior or coordination with other processes/store instances; [development evidence](development.md#atomic-local-profile-replacement--2026-10-06) separates executed results and limits.

C4 extends the existing provisioning suites with exact normalized responses from real scripts, successful profile parsing through upstream Config, strict nested/missing/wrong-type shapes, safe remote categories/recovery, nonzero script failure dispatch, secret-safe logs/store corruption and legacy/null running migration. Command tests require Bash in the JVM test environment: fake id/sudo/script stubs exercise both constructed dispatch branches and preserve hostile argument/path bytes without root or SSH. Native running/stopped/unknown and missing/malformed counters are separate Bash cases. Mapper controls cover stopped, legacy/unknown, stale/future and threshold observations; ONLINE does not establish connectivity. [Development evidence](development.md#c4-provisioning-contracts-and-observed-health--2026-10-06) records actual runs.

The separate [Bash provisioning safety suite](../scripts/tests/provisioning_safety_test.sh) exercises real scripts with fake commands and synthetic keys in private `/tmp` fixtures. It requires namespace UID 0 mapped to an unprivileged host UID and refuses host root. Coverage includes unmanaged/symlink preservation, input injection, interface/host lock races, staged write/apply/rollback faults, exact runtime key/port/peer/routes/PSKs, malformed peer metadata, forced reinstall ordering, private archives/backups, stopped-interface controls, partial purge, post-commit cleanup and TERM recovery. [Development evidence](development.md#c2c3-offline-provisioning-safety--2026-10-06) records executed totals and limits; these are separate from JUnit inventory. No installed WireGuard/systemd/apt command, SSH session or live network is exercised.

## Concrete SSH transport controls — C11

### API24/25 ECDSA compatibility controls — #143

The 2026-10-18 (Europe/Paris) focused WSL JDK21 gate ran `Api24EcdsaTest`, `JschRemoteShellClientTest` and `SshTestServerDisconnectTest`: 24 + 31 + 5 = 60 tests passed, with zero failures/errors/skips and zero Detekt findings. The new class runs its compatibility controls in Robolectric API24/25 and its exclusion control in API26/28. It exercises real JSch/Apache MINA loopback authentication using host JDK crypto; it does not execute Android's native provider. The retained gate receipt binds all four changed source/test files to the independently reviewed source, including the declaration-local `ThrowsCount` suppression that preserves three explicit signature rejection guards.

Coverage includes P-256/P-384/P-521 signatures in both directions against JSch, all three pinned ECDSA host curves with password authentication, all three traditional EC PEM curves with plain/encrypted private authentication, unchanged RSA-SHA2 authentication and API26+ verifier/identity selection. Fixed wire vectors cover high-bit/leading-zero integers and P-521 long DER lengths. Wrong signature algorithms, EC coordinate widths, negative/empty integers, truncated/trailing frames, wrong pin before authentication and wrong EC passphrase before socket creation are rejected. Identity removal checks parsed-scalar clearing and prevents further signing; an authenticated EC command cancellation checks socket closure, session drain, reader-thread settlement and terminal reuse refusal. Owned scalar/export/DER arrays are clearable; caller Strings, opaque JCA keys and internal BC/immutable numeric copies do not establish complete heap zeroization.

The first gate stopped at configuration because the new isolated worktree lacked its ignored SDK path. The second reached source preparation but Detekt rejected the decoder's three explicit throws against its two-throw limit. Both failures and their logs remain retained. Reusing the existing local SDK path and adding one justified declaration-scoped suppression produced the passing third gate; no assertion, protocol guard, provider/global configuration, dependency, algorithm policy or timeout was relaxed. The later [API24 E1/API28 F1 matrix](reviews/2026-10-18-native-ssh-acceptance.md#api24-e1-and-api28-f1-ecdsa-compatibility--2026-10-18) supplies separate native API24 and API28 transport proof on the same source-bound acceptance APK. The separate [API25 G2 cohort](reviews/2026-10-18-native-ssh-acceptance.md#api25-g1-host-failure-and-g2-compatibility--2026-10-18) accepts the same named isolated matrix on the unchanged original APK. Physical/OEM providers, release shrinking, installed production integration and wider #143 acceptance remain separate. The [architecture decision](architecture.md#reuse-platform-ecdsa-through-api2425-key-encodings--143-2026-10-18) and [transport contract](backend-contracts.md#concrete-ssh-transport--c11) own the implementation and memory boundaries.

### Client disconnect diagnostics — #143

`SshTestServerDisconnectTest` defines fixed-category/privacy controls and a real public SSHD callback receipt using an explicit synthetic reason-3 disconnect. Its SSHD client verifies the actual expected host fingerprint, reaches WAIT_AUTH without authenticating, then waits for packet-write completion and the actual server callback before client closure. `JschRemoteShellClientTest.changedPinCannotAuthenticateEvenWhenLegacyTrustFlagIsEnabled` separately retains production changed-pin rejection and zero authentication attempts; it does not require a remote callback. The test-only `SshTestServer` owner observes incoming disconnects before session closure, ignores locally initiated notifications and preserves the first remote observation. Only seen/reason/typed category survive; descriptions, their hashes, proposals, credentials, keys and stacks are never retained. JSch 2.28.8 attempts to send the caught connect exception in a reason-3 disconnect before the production transport replaces it with its fixed failure. Finite classifications cover signature refusal, known negotiation components and whitelisted direct/wrapped Java exception categories; unsupported text stays `OTHER_REDACTED`, absent callbacks stay `NOT_OBSERVED`. Packet-write failure or early closure can prevent receipt, so absence cannot identify a cause.

The companion ignored Peer snapshots these public fixture observations from its existing failure finally. This adds no product transport/configuration, logger, crypto warmup, timeout, authentication, native success control or retry. Focused JVM execution requires the separately authorized Gradle gate; static preparation alone is not a passed test. The [API24 D2 failure](reviews/2026-10-18-native-ssh-acceptance.md#api24-disconnect-diagnostic-d2--2026-10-18) now retains an actual incoming reason-3 `NO_SUCH_ALGORITHM` category; the exact missing algorithm, failing call site and cause were not observed. Post-failure primitive initialization is not KEX/signature proof. Existing source/artifact/native scopes below remain distinct.

`JschRemoteShellClientTest` drives mwiede JSch 2.28.8 against test-only Apache MINA SSHD 2.20.0 with synthetic password/keys and a temporary virtual SFTP filesystem. The peer executes fixture responses without a host shell. Coverage includes lazy creation, numeric endpoints and required canonical pins before socket allocation, changed-key denial before authentication, password and traditional encrypted/unencrypted RSA/ECDSA PEM auth, RSA/ECDSA host keys, refusal of SHA1-only host-key and forced-CTR MAC peers, independent stdout/stderr caps, nonzero/missing status and real SFTP bytes/chmod denial. IPv6 loopback has an explicit unavailable-bind skip rather than a hostname fallback.

Controlled sockets and entered real channels cover cancellation/guard timeout before connect returns, blocked banner reads/writes, withheld channel-open confirmation, hung exec/SFTP and concurrent close. Assertions bound wall-clock completion, require closed raw sockets and drained JSch session threads, preserve caller cancellation and unexpected Error identity through coroutine stack recovery, and forbid reconnect after terminal close. Real-reader Error controls cover channel-open waits, entered-command polling, entered SFTP WRITE acknowledgments and an idle reader followed only by close; raw close defects cannot replace original failures or self-suppress. Refused loopback and secret-bearing injected IO/key inputs check fixed cause-free driver failures. `GuardedRemoteShellClientTest` separately proves wrapper timeout ownership. `VpsProvisioningRequestTest` accepts the current mktemp path with terminal newlines and rejects blank, unrelated, malformed, whitespace, traversal and multiline replies before any mkdir/upload/deletion. Its shell fixture maps the valid virtual remote path into an owned local temporary directory, retaining hostile-argument and root/sudo quoting controls without privileged filesystem writes. The suite proves local close after successful/failed/invalid temp creation and original cancellation/Errors surviving distinct or same-instance remote/local cleanup failures. A nested coroutine control checks primary Error preservation and local close, without promising copied secondary metadata. Existing logging tests keep raw response/cleanup payloads out of logs.

These checks use JDK 21/Robolectric SDK 28, not Android native crypto/network execution. The separately bound native trials below accept their named API24/25/28 compatibility and physical API34 key-loading/exec/SFTP/cancellation controls. Wider key/provider/OEM/network coverage, ordinary VPN/lockdown routing, native protection, mutation/progress/profile wiring and actual disposable-server postconditions remain under open [#143](https://github.com/novakin/vpn0wn-android/issues/143). Read-only credential capture/reuse controls are separate below. No APK, ADB, external SSH or privileged server mutation belongs to these JVM checks. `LicensesActivityTest` retains complete per-notice accessibility text for the three source-pinned JSch notices; no new device or distributed-artifact license acceptance follows.

### Scoped native SSH transport acceptance — 2026-10-18

The separate [physical API34 SSH record](reviews/2026-10-18-native-ssh-acceptance.md) accepts a Pixel 7 Pro trial of the production JSch factory/guarded transport in an isolated arm64 package. Actual source `13fcc23f`, tree `1bc25253`, 637 unchanged source inputs and eight reviewed harness inputs are bound to fresh Build `20261018-021804-8454c4`, Peer `20261018-022417-883734` and the audited/installed APK hash. Seven native checks pass: pinned RSA/password exec with separate output/status and client reuse, SFTP success, ECDSA-host-key exec, wrong-pin and wrong-password refusal, entered authenticated-command cancellation, and terminal socket/reader teardown. Six independent host postconditions verify exact UTF-8/0700 output, the entered cancellation fixture, zero authentication/commands on the wrong-pin peer, exact authentication counts, drained sessions and stopped workers. The peer producer exits zero; all ten cleanup/preservation checks pass with no errors. These are complementary observations of one bounded trial, not thirteen transport scenarios.

The record retains the earlier host compilation failure, its independently reviewed three-call Java Files API correction, fresh build/audit and failed-attempt cleanup. A [separate private-key follow-up](reviews/2026-10-18-native-ssh-acceptance.md#native-private-key-acceptance--2026-10-18) accepts plain/encrypted traditional RSA2048 and EC P-256 authentication on physical API34 at source `a0d39751`, safe wrong-passphrase refusal before the socket-creator callback/connect, and terminal teardown: seven native controls, four complementary host postconditions and ten cleanup checks passed. Its different isolated APK/source, retained preflight HOLD/correction, actual bytes/UID/producer exits and independent ultra reviews stay distinct from the older password/SFTP/cancellation trial; the latter is not promoted to newer transport. Those physical trials changed no production source or live server. Beyond the later API24/25/28 compatibility cohorts below, wider key/provider/physical/OEM coverage, IPv6/external paths, blocked connect/write/upload boundaries, wizard/production-app integration, VPN/lockdown routing and live mutation/client traffic remain open under #143. The separate API24/28 storage tests alone do not establish transport acceptance.

The [API24 E1/API28 F1 follow-up](reviews/2026-10-18-native-ssh-acceptance.md#api24-e1-and-api28-f1-ecdsa-compatibility--2026-10-18) executes production source `54399d3`/tree `c5965629` through one fresh audited x86_64 acceptance APK, reused unchanged for API28. Each one-shot guest passes fifteen native controls and six complementary host postconditions: RSA-host/password exec/SFTP/refusal/cancellation/teardown, all three ECDSA host curves and six plain/encrypted EC private-key cases. Direct controller/Peer and inner exits are zero; complete native reports have code -1, and all six cleanup/five shutdown checks pass. Different actual-runtime ultra reviews pass 52 E1 and 50 F1 checks. E1's additional post-run Windows snapshot keeps its own scope; F1 uses controller-recorded absence/shared-owner evidence without a supplemental live query. D2 remains FAIL with its cause unobserved. Later signed integration preserves the two production/two test blobs; historical 657-file input equality belongs to the runtime Git tree, not current documentation or a rebuilt final-head APK. The record owns exact artifact/review hashes, preserved failures and remaining gates.

The separate [API25 G2 cohort](reviews/2026-10-18-native-ssh-acceptance.md#api25-g1-host-failure-and-g2-compatibility--2026-10-18) reuses the original E1 source/APK and passes the same fifteen native/six complementary host controls, with direct controller/Peer/inner exits zero, exact native completion/settlement ordering, 129 expected command results and six fixed private-fixture markers. All six cleanup and all five shutdown facts pass, including retained userdata; the different actual-runtime ultra is accepted. G1 remains FAILED before Peer/Gradle/native execution with checked cleanup; a G2 collector-only path/SHA256 projection correction is not a runtime rerun. This adds one isolated API25 matrix, no API25 RSA-private-key, production Application/wizard or wider #143 acceptance.

### Protected SSH credential controls — C11

The 2026-10-15 temporary native Robolectric preview renderer retained 64 EN/FR, portrait/short-landscape, day/night, 1×/2× text captures. Normal-text notes and controls had no measured fit/reachability findings; inspected note captures show the then-truthful preview-only copy. The full renderer failed with 60 repeated enlarged-text findings: its 38dp authentication tabs clipped 32px text in 22px content, and the 640×320dp form viewport collapsed beneath the header/tracker/footer. The later scalable-layout repair addresses those owners; [current output limits](ui-redesign-roadmap.md#fidelity-gaps--remaining) still require fresh proof for new read-only wizard states. The temporary runner and images remain historical ignored evidence, outside default checks. That slice's ADB inventory found no phone/emulator; no native Keystore run or APK build followed from it.

`VpsCredentialFileTest` uses the real JVM AES-GCM implementation with synthetic keys and owned temporary files, independently decrypts the authenticated header format and exercises fresh IVs, missing/unavailable keys, tampering/version mismatch, read denial and staged write/sync/close/rename failures. It checks ciphertext-only persistence and prior-file preservation. `VpsServerCredentialTest` covers password/private-key/passphrase/pin round trips, strict current identity, unrelated records, corrupt/key-lost storage, metadata/credential rollback, orphan re-addition and admitted/waiting cancellation. Existing store, transfer, logging and concrete transport suites remain affected controls.

Manager transfer controls must prove that new-ID orphan conflicts precede restoration-marker/profile writes, identical fleet imports retain current credentials, ordinary ZIP bytes contain no SSH password/key/passphrase/pin, and export remains usable when the vault cannot be read. Preserve existing commit/rollback and cancellation meaning under tunnel → fleet → DataStore admission. Neither source tests nor absence from an explicitly enumerated ZIP proves Android backup/device-transfer behavior. The separate [native API-24/28 storage record](reviews/2026-10-15-native-ssh-credential-storage-acceptance.md) verifies seven controls on the same isolated acceptance APK: fresh storage, Keystore roundtrip, private ciphertext, normal atomic replacement, unrelated-record preservation, tamper refusal and lost-key refusal. Beyond the separate [saved-access B4 cohort](reviews/2026-10-19-native-saved-access-acceptance.md), wider native capture/reuse/removal UI and Android transport, broader Keystore lifecycle, backup/crash/physical and live-server gates remain under #143. These source controls themselves establish no APK/device or real-server result; native admission, execution and cleanup retain their separate recorded scope.

### Confirmed SSH host trust and field presentation — #143

**2026-10-19 source and scoped local verification complete; [T2/T3 API28 native acceptance](reviews/2026-10-19-native-ssh-host-trust-acceptance.md) is separate from wider pending gates.** The [contract](backend-contracts.md#confirmed-ssh-host-trust--143) and [superseding decision](architecture.md#confirm-and-retain-ssh-host-trust--143-2026-10-19) own behavior. Earlier manual-pin transport/storage/Detect cohorts retain their original revisions; they do not verify discovery, confirmation, the enriched trust snapshot or current field output.

Verify the real JSch discovery entry point against a synthetic SSH peer: key exchange yields the expected canonical pin with no username/password/client-key authentication, exec or SFTP; invalid endpoint, deadline/cancellation and cleanup failures retain their correct outcomes. A discovered key authorizes nothing until the exact native confirmation is accepted. Cancel, stale callbacks, navigation and altered input must not save trust or start authentication. Confirmed trust must be durable before authentication, remain after wrong credentials/cancellation/reopen, and be reused for the same canonical numeric host/port across usernames, fleet IDs and equivalent IPv6 spelling. Different ports remain separate. Changed keys and concurrent conflicting trust must be blocked without replacement or bypass.

Storage controls must cover compatible legacy credential-pin loading, strict new host-key rows, same-host conflicting legacy pins, corrupt/key-lost preservation, failed trust commits, confirmed credential clearing and fleet deletion retaining trust, and save/load agreement between credential and host pin. Preserve existing admitted-write cancellation settlement and private/no-backup storage limits. Older readers reject enriched snapshots; no destructive downgrade/reset is part of validation.

The focused verification used Debian WSL, Java 21 and SDK-28 Robolectric native graphics. The transport/store gate passed its real synthetic-peer discovery and persistence controls while exposing stale workflow/layout fixtures; the subsequent 44-test ViewModel gate passed the corrected IPv6 identity and failed-trust-write controls. A 75-test layout/dialog/removal/rotation gate and a 116-test adjacent backup/comparison/endpoint/create/disclosure/restore gate passed with no failures or skips and zero Detekt findings. After the final Detect-status wrapping correction, the affected 12-test layout class and Detekt passed again. These gates overlap; their counts are not a unique-suite total. Source checkpoint `65207328` was followed by documentation-only integration `718b9c06`; the final 12-test run covers the subsequent local Detect-wrapping delta. The delivering PR binds the final tested source/head, full CI and review anchoring.

The [first full CI run](https://github.com/novakin/vpn0wn-android/actions/runs/39230318391) on `3e36a494` ran 1,611 tests with 49 stale-prerequisite failures: 34 in `VpsInstallApplyTest`, 13 in `VpsFreshRestoreFragmentTest`, one in `VpsFreshRestoreLayoutTest` and one in `VpsNavigationTest`. Four test-only corrections make install/restore setup explicitly confirm discovered host trust, confirm a replacement endpoint separately, and establish previously accepted trust before navigation's blocked Detect. Existing denial, admission, cancellation and cleanup assertions remain. The corrected four-class rerun passed all 56 tests with no failures, errors or skips, and Detekt passed with zero findings. Final-head CI remains a separate merge gate; no new installed/native result follows.

Earlier failed runs remain retained: native underline alpha rounding required a bounded pixel comparison; an 8dp padding attempt left only 2dp descender clearance; French 200% confirmation text clipped; and old fixtures attempted now-forbidden pin replacement. The final native padding is 12dp with an asserted minimum 4dp painted descender gap, the action is **Trust server** / **Faire confiance**, the tint matcher rejects nearly transparent pixels, and adjacent tests exercise retained trust, replacement refusal and missing-access races without bypassing the store. Independent ultra also found and verified correction of saved IPv6 endpoint-spelling drift; trust identity alone is canonicalized. Detect status now wraps its complete runtime-not-checked qualification.

**Author UI verdict: satisfied within the retained synthetic native matrix.** Interface Design, Design System, Frontend Design and UI/UX Pro Max informed the approved EditField/focus intent, existing palette/type roles, native dialog/disabled controls and full-text reachability. Author inspection and independent ultra review cover 59 original rendered views: 24 dialog top/fingerprint/bottom frames across EN/FR, day/night qualifiers, 320×640dp 1×LTR and 640×320dp 2×forced RTL; eight editor crops; and 27 contextual form, unavailable-action, saved-access and forwarding frames. Tests assert actual locale/font/direction, completed pre-draw, reachable full dialog text/actions, 48dp targets and no trust/authentication during presentation. Enlarged short layouts require native scrolling. Existing form matrices retain broader orientation/direction coverage. These are manual native-view Robolectric renders with synthetic data, not installed-device screenshots. Real IME/TalkBack, Android discovery/Keystore/restart, provider/OEM and live-server acceptance remain separate; no new APK/install, live connection or production-readiness result follows.

**T2/T3 acceptance boundary:** the [native record](reviews/2026-10-19-native-ssh-host-trust-acceptance.md) covers two API28 English portrait dark-theme synthetic-peer cohorts on one unchanged source/APK for discovery/Cancel/confirmation, wrong-password trust retention, fresh-process Keystore reopening, saved reuse/Clear and changed-key refusal. Acceptance requires complete native and complementary host evidence, settled controller/Peer wrappers, author and independent review of original pixels, and checked cleanup/shutdown; T1 phase passes alone do not establish it. Static visual acceptance combines T2 saved-success/reuse originals with T3 settled-dialog output; each cohort retains its capture omissions, and motion remains unverified. Physical/OEM, release, IME/TalkBack, broader output/API/key/network coverage, admitted-operation cancellation and live-server workflows remain pending; #143 stays open.

### Read-only Detect/Plan wizard controls — #143

The separate [2026-10-19 saved-access B4 record](reviews/2026-10-19-native-saved-access-acceptance.md) adds one API28 x86_64 same-server password-reuse, Clear-dialog Cancel/confirm and post-Clear missing-access refusal cohort on frozen source `12e46e3d`, reusing its reviewed B3 APK. Eleven native and six complementary host facts, checked cleanup/shutdown and five original English portrait dark-theme targets pass. It preserves B1/B2 failures and B3's passing raw receipts with failed collection. Target-feedback pixel satisfaction does not accept the user-observed field text/underline spacing gap, complete-screen polish, in-flight cancellation, recreation, manual status refresh, later AWG source or live-server/VPN behavior; #143 remains open.

The separate [2026-10-18 native wizard Detect record](reviews/2026-10-18-native-wizard-detect-acceptance.md) accepts one fresh API28 x86_64 positive password-Detect cohort through the production Application/default Fragment/ViewModel/client/Socket and real Keystore. Exactly six native facts, instrumentation code `-1`, six complementary host facts, all 80 expected command receipts, pre/post sysfs/service/diagnostic observations, six cleanup and five shutdown facts pass. Different independent ultra verified all 312 original evidence bindings and both unequal 1080×1920 English portrait dark-theme originals. It reuses the unchanged V7 APK and fixed-response synthetic peer; uploaded production scripts are byte-checked but not executed on a real server. A4 remains failed; V5/A2 and V6/A3 native frames remain unconfirmed, and offline formatter corrections add no runtime rerun. Closed canonical admission gates preserve the original consumed-execution copies/index. Wider workflow/failure/cancellation/recreation, key/network/API/OEM/physical, output/accessibility, VPN and live-server acceptance remain open under #143. The source/JVM controls below retain their separate scopes.

`VpsNavigationTest` starts the actual `MainActivity`, clicks Home's Servers action, enters Add server and checks Back restoration on phone/tablet layouts. A second tablet Servers click must preserve the existing fleet owner. Selecting tablet tunnel rows from Servers must display the selected detail from initially empty, selected-detail and editor contexts, including reselection of the same tunnel; the removed wizard must cancel, clear secrets and drain its remote operation. Back from Servers restores existing detail/editor state on phone/tablet, retaining the original final-detail Back policy. Recreation with a selected tunnel must preserve the Servers Fragment, retained ViewModel and draft. Retain the existing Home native output matrix for header reachability, complete summary text and 48dp controls after adding this action; source wiring alone does not prove its rendered fit.

The ViewModel IPv6 regression uses the packaged `common.sh` validator: a bare numeric IPv6 endpoint is rejected while the automatically bracketed default is accepted. It then checks the actual client Plan command/result with that default; a response-only fake cannot establish the endpoint's script compatibility.

Fresh Detect controls change the SSH host and the configured detected endpoint independently, replacing earlier generated defaults while preserving an explicit endpoint override. Clearing an override restores derivation on the next Detect; rejected busy edits cannot freeze an old default. Fixture teardown releases blocked remote/local work, cancels and joins retained ViewModel jobs and then cancels/joins DataStore before resetting Main. The admitted-save control releases its private blocker in `finally`, including assertion failures.

`VpsProvisioningViewModelTest` injects the existing remote-shell factory into the actual client/owner with synthetic responses and a real temporary encrypted credential store. Require no credential/fleet save before authenticated Detect, explicit host-trust persistence before authentication, rejected pin/auth failure with no Detect advance or credential save but retained accepted trust, numeric-address/port admission before discovery and canonical-pin admission before authenticated transport, exact Plan arguments/result matching, unsupported-server gating, invalidation after SSH/network edits, saved reuse without UI secret disclosure or redundant ciphertext rewrite, changed saved-pin rejection before Plan, missing saved access and explicit clearing with host-trust/metadata preservation. Fail metadata and credential writes separately; retain the actual detection result and distinguish metadata-only/unverified saves. Cancellation must drain the client, reject late publication and clear copied passwords; duplicate operations cannot start.

`VpsProvisioningWorkflowTest` uses real Fragment controls with that injected factory. Verify actual Detect/Plan result publication, native Apply confirmation with no install before acceptance and no add-peer calls, draft/operation retention across rotation, absent secrets in serialized instance state, permanent-removal cancellation and saved-access reuse/clear confirmation Cancel/success. `VpsProvisioningLayoutTest` retains EN/FR, LTR/RTL, both modes, portrait/short landscape and 1×/2× fitting/reachability controls. Incomplete credentials stay at SSH; valid synthetic credentials execute the actual client Detect/Plan and reach Apply review without confirming installation. Require complete painted tracker labels, equal full-width columns when they fit, horizontal access to every enlarged column and automatic active-stage visibility. A separate test invokes the actual tracker renderer with each of its five stage inputs; it establishes stage geometry, not an Import transition or completed installation. Retain localized icon names, 48dp repaired targets, native button/radio semantics and interaction foregrounds, plus numeric port glyph visibility under RTL. `VpsSectionHeaderLayoutTest` exercises the unchanged shared header owner with real factory strings across the same layout matrix. The [fresh credential/result rendering record](#scoped-c12-credential-rendering--2026-10-16) covers forms and successful injected results; remaining install/network/error-state output and complete visual acceptance stay separate. These tests add no real SSH, root/server mutation or native Keystore proof; the PR owns executed results and independent review.

The historical layout candidate at PR #194 head `6524e3c3d1b88879805e9d081da51908d0baf0b7` retains its [scoped review](reviews/2026-10-15-uiux-review.md), five passing native tests, zero-finding Detekt and 128 PNGs in the separate layout worktree. The settled-error control uses a thread-bound immediate Main dispatcher with the test scheduler for worker-to-main dispatch, matching Android's thread-affinity behavior. A synthetic failed client operation is blocked until its initiating click returns, forcing suspension before the failure returns from the actual I/O context; both its busy-with-error and idle-with-the-same-error emissions must be observed, and the Fragment must render the busy error before replacing it at settlement and revealing the final bounds. This covers immediate collectors as well as the ordinary StandardTestDispatcher's conflation, without real SSH. Saved-access confirmation controls explicitly dispatch Android's posted dialog callbacks before awaiting store outcomes; fixture teardown still drains every owner.

#### Repeatable user-creation controls

Verify canonical unique name and exact-object confirmation; captured full server/SSH pin and independently observed key; same-target install/add exclusion; durable lost/malformed/cancelled fences after owner recreation; complete parser-to-encrypted-vault round trip; private receipt settlement before IO-return cancellation; copied-password/client drain; file failure with local-only retry; successful vault/failed intent commit with exact readback recovery; pin/record drift retaining an orphan while suppressing current-owner claims; exact post-add peer/address/network identity and no fabricated traffic health. Known pretransaction rejections may clear only the checked unretained intent; unknown outcomes never auto-retry or reconstruct a private profile from public status.

Drive actual Fragment Review on B while A has a valid unsaved receipt: failed A retention must show B's storage failure without losing A's copy/fence, and successful retry must reach the first B confirmation despite marker-only DataStore writes. Check the encrypted profile remains under A, no B profile/admission/add exists before confirmation, and B cannot show A's creation result. Give A an actual successful add receipt with cleanup guidance, then navigate/refresh/Review B and return to A: B must not inherit the warning, while A's unchanged SSH target keeps guidance through ordinary metadata updates. Repeat with actual RESTORED/INCOMPLETE install feedback, reject a changed SSH target, and retain direct same-attempt UNCHANGED/RESTORED reconciliation and owner-consumption controls. Public Detect still discards the known receipt as documented.

Native Add/review output still needs actual EN/FR small portrait/landscape, supported themes, large text/RTL, keyboard/inset clearance, labels/TalkBack, focus and 48dp touch proof under the existing matrix. Verify stale dialog dismissal on navigation/rebuild/stop/destruction and current-record-only result presentation. QR/export were unavailable at that creation revision and are not accepted by its output matrix; the later authenticated profile-access candidate requires separate proof. Real Keystore and explicitly authorized disposable-server add/retained-client traffic remain separate.

Verify an actual new pinned read despite a cached snapshot; exact fleet/SSH/server-key/client-key lookup; full record/pin drift during remote/local waits; peer removal/name reuse/key/address/DNS/endpoint/port/interface/CIDR drift; missing/corrupt/key-lost vault; known stopped status without invented health; SSH failure without offline fallback; one admitted consumer; copied-password/client cleanup before consumption; cancellation before/after IO return; redacted StateFlow and no config-bearing Bundle/saved state.

Drive actual native actions with authentication success, no-lock policy, failed sample, terminal failure/Cancel, duplicate callbacks/taps, stale view/selection/navigation and destroyed owner. Change administrator export policy before click, during authentication/remote work and at output acquisition; QR/file must expose no unauthorized bytes. Independently decode the displayed QR to the complete canonical payload, including private key and PSK; inspect secure-window flags and dismissal cleanup. Encoding overflow must remain an error with export guidance. API-24/28 Fragment registration, denied/retried storage permission, permission-before-fresh-read ordering, non-overwrite and partial-output cleanup need controls. Compare exported bytes with the same complete profile and import the actual file through existing `TunnelImporter`, including a 32-character VPS peer name; the exported basename must remain a valid tunnel name within 15 characters.

Import must require explicit editable naming, perform another fresh check on retry, preserve existing names/data after collision/save failure and create exactly one DOWN tunnel after success. Busy controls must prevent duplicate creation. Drive a genuinely blocked status read: checking feedback, native disabled semantics and a 48dp Cancel must remain observable; current Cancel settles the owned read, while an old detached Cancel cannot retire a later action. Known export restrictions render QR/file/header controls disabled while local import remains available. Fresh SSH/status and unavailable-profile failures before manager admission must show the corresponding fixed error inside the still-current naming form without losing its name or creating a tunnel. Cancellation after manager admission retains the manager's existing settlement semantics; do not manufacture a successful UI receipt or retry automatically. Inspect normal/small portrait/landscape, both system modes, EN/FR, 2× text, RTL, native keyboard and TalkBack, including QR close/name-input reachability. Existing SSH-vault evidence does not establish the profile vault, new output or live client traffic.

#### Repeatable source controls

- Exact confirmation is required: review/request/Cancel/forged or stale tokens cannot install. SSH/network edits and changed fleet identity reject the old plan/confirmation. Existing configs and overwrite plans stay unavailable; actual requests contain no `--force`.
- Destination confirmation retains the reviewed title/details and uses the explicit Install/Installer positive action in EN/FR. Material's native footer can stack and scroll actions when localized or enlarged text needs space. Preserve the initial frame, then use actual native scroll/focus to reveal each complete action and verify its 48dp target, painted text and bounds inside both footer viewport and dialog. Inspect normal narrow portrait plus 2× narrow RTL, landscape and both system modes; do not reduce text/targets, enlarge the test viewport or demand simultaneous visibility from a scrolling footer.
- Save authenticated A, edit to B with B’s pin, and fail B’s metadata save through both credential-identity conflict and ordinary I/O. Actual read-only Detect/Plan remain available, but no confirmation, install or A/B admission is allowed. The positive control compares the frozen expected record, actual client SSH/pin, durable target/request and final fleet SSH identity. Direct store admission of another actual target must reject before writing.
- The real Fragment failed-save control proceeds through actual injected Detect/Plan to Apply with no observed config or pending operation. Require localized current-record/fresh-Detect guidance, no existing-config/pending claim, disabled Apply, unchanged fleet and no install/admission. Existing-config and pending explanations still require their observed state; presentation does not weaken persistence admission.
- Normal completion requires the actual install response, valid public identity encoding, matching running status with empty initial peer inventory and expected-record metadata/admission commit. Status failures, malformed results and changed records cannot publish success. Keep copied-password cleanup and operation/client settlement.
- Leave while durable admission is blocked, then release the write. Require inactive/cleared wizard state, preserved pending intent, no install command and drained client/password resources. Admission callback state checks/publication stay on Main; noncancellable persistence still settles. Cancellation during install or status verification also retains durable pending intent. A new owner reuses saved access and discovers that intent before any new Apply; remove/recreate of a fleet record on the same SSH target cannot bypass it. Corrupt pending state fails before remote mutation and remains excluded from ordinary fleet/app-data transfer.
- During blocked post-install status and delayed reconciliation status, remove or replace the captured record’s SSH identity, then release the response. Require settled `CONNECTION_CHANGED`, no success/peer publication/metadata overwrite, preserved admission and drained/cleared operation resources. Repeat removal/replacement before the reconciliation pending lookup; no new network client may start. These are shared-store ownership controls, not a claim that fleet removal currently has a UI action.
- A lost-response existing-config reconciliation publishes OBSERVED, not INSTALLED, with no retry. Missing/mismatched/unknown state retains the marker. An actually received RESTORED/UNCHANGED result for the still-unsettled attempt plus NOT_INSTALLED and reconciliation's internal supported absent-config Detect can remove that known failed marker only by returning to Detect with no Plan. Positive controls cover both receipts and require a new Plan and explicit confirmation. Both absent and observed/verified settlements clear recovery evidence. Starting public wizard Detect clears it before discovering pending state; process death likewise cannot supply a lost receipt.
- With two actual ViewModel owners sharing the same store, let A receive RESTORED/UNCHANGED and settle its absent attempt, then let B confirm the same unchanged target/request and lose its reply. A's new Detect followed by NOT_INSTALLED reconciliation must preserve B's unknown marker, UNCERTAIN state and exactly two explicitly requested install commands, without another internal Detect. Separate controls require fresh wizard Detect to discard a still-unsettled receipt and observed settlement to consume the earlier receipt. No marker generation or format change is involved.
- Admit network request A, lose its response, then edit/generate advisory Plan B before reconciling actual A. The actual Fragment must show A's observed endpoint/port/CIDR/DNS beside OBSERVED feedback, with B absent from that readout and exactly one install command. Repeat with a new owner whose default Plan differs from retained non-default A; metadata and the retained status snapshot must still describe A. Verified/observed presentation never falls back to a different Plan when its observation is unavailable.
- Preserve fixed structured recovery/cleanup feedback without raw diagnostics or paths. Retain the actual Fragment native confirmation, Cancel and rotation-detached-positive-action controls; a fresh confirmed dialog can drive the injected actual client path. No add-peer/private-profile/QR success is introduced.

The 2026-10-16 prerequisite source slice keeps Android user/profile wiring outside scope. `VpsProvisioningRequestTest` checks mandatory guarded arguments through root/sudo branches, invalid identity rejection before transport, exact response name/key/action, cleanup/close and no retry after mismatched receipts. `ProvisioningJsonTest` checks required canonical mutation keys, nonblank names, strict removed/revoked/archive fields and safe existing error categories.
